# Cenapro Module (Tenant #2 — CI / Cebu)

## Purpose
Top-level route `/cenapro` for the **second tenant** on the Blackwood platform: the CI / Cebu charcoal company ("Cenapro"). Five screens over the dedicated `cenapro` Postgres schema: an **editable** Production ledger (the app is now the maintaining file — operators add/edit/delete production rows here instead of in Excel), a Flec Inventory view whose **STARTING (opening) balances are now editable + append-only + backtrackable** (the rest of the inventory view — closing balances + movement ledger — stays computed/read-only), and the **QC pair** — an editable QC Ledger for CCC's partner lab results plus a read-only QC Breakdown. Fully decoupled from the ICTC / Davao tenant — shares zero tables, types, or code.

> **Tenant/Domain Module (Cenapro):** Cebu-specific.
> - **Production (`/cenapro/production`)** is an **editable Industrial-Spreadsheet ledger** — inline cell editing, dropdowns for categorical columns, add/delete rows, paste-from-Excel, dirty tracking + single Save/Discard. It writes through the auto-updatable `public.cenapro_production_events` VIEW (see "Production write path" below).
> - **Flec Inventory (`/cenapro/inventory`)** mirrors the Excel "PC WHSE" sheet: an **editable STARTING block** (opening flec count per grade × side, append-only, with full history) on top of computed read-only closing balances + the show-your-math movement ledger. Only the opening balances are written; everything below them is re-derived in SQL (see "Opening-balance write path" below).
> - **QC Ledger (`/cenapro/qc`)** is the **editable** entry grid for CCC's partner lab results (BD · ASH · GRIT · MC), one reading per source + warehouse per day, written through `cenapro_save_analysis_sample` with optimistic concurrency — **plus, since 2026-08-03, the receipt WEIGHT itself** (`WT kg`, per individual draw, through `cenapro_update_event_weight` with compare-and-set), so a mistyped weight is fixed where it is noticed, **and (2026-08-03) ADDING a partner draw** through `cenapro_add_partner_draw`, from a docked "Add partner draws" composer that opens once per paper slip and stays open across saves — partner draws ONLY, because the partner's paper slip and CI's bagging sheet are two different documents; flec bagging entry stays in the Production ledger and the RPC refuses it. Entry moved OUT of that composer and INTO the rows on 2026-08-04, and on the same day's second pass every draft cell but `PLANT` became typable (BD/ASH/GRIT/MC included), the two date cells became free text that parses like Excel, and add mode gained an always-available **Cancel adding**. **On the third pass (2026-08-04) `PLANT` stopped being the exception — end to end.** `cenapro_add_partner_draw` gained an optional `p_plant` and the cell became the same `SelectCell` + badge dropdown the Production ledger has always had on that column: derived from SRC by default (ghosted), overridable when the partner's slip says otherwise, `— follow SRC` to go back, and `p_plant` sent **only** for a real override. The same pass left **both date cells blank on every new row**. See "PLANT is typable" below. **QC Breakdown (`/cenapro/qc/breakdown`)** is its read-only reading surface. See "CCC / QC Analysis — THE LIVE FEATURE" below.
> - **RC Deliveries (`/cenapro/deliveries`) — LIVE since 2026-08-04.** Cenapro's raw-charcoal receipts (the analogue of ICTC's `public.deliveries`), sourced from the "RC 2026" tab of Cenapro's RC workbook: **971 receipts** + 244 moisture sub-samples in a NEW `rc_`-prefixed island that shares zero dimensions with production (991 were imported; the 22 duplicates were deleted on 2026-08-04 and 2 receipts have since been created in the app). Schema, read model, write RPCs, importer **and the grid** are all live and verified. Since 2026-08-05 every write is trailed in `rc_delivery_audit`. See **`deliveries/CONTEXT.md`** for the screen and "RC Deliveries — DATA LAYER" below for the schema. **Liquidation** (assigning cheques to receipts) is the feature this exists to support. **Steps 1–3 shipped 2026-08-05** (`.agents/prompts/liquidation-feature.md` §7): the audit trail, supplier subgroups, and — Step 3 — **banks, bank accounts, payments and the per-supplier running balance** (`cenapro.view_rc_supplier_balance`, the first thing that can answer "what do we owe BRIX right now"). **Step 3b followed 2026-08-06: APPEND-ONLY supplier opening balances** (`cenapro.rc_supplier_opening_balance`), because the balance summed the whole year and back-entering seven months of cheques is not realistic — Renzo states what is outstanding as of a date and the system counts forward. **Step 4 shipped 2026-08-06 (DATA LAYER ONLY, no UI): ALLOCATION** — `cenapro.rc_payment_allocation` (many-to-many, amount on the edge), `view_rc_delivery_settlement` (`settlement_status`, where `unpriced` never collapses into `settled`), `view_rc_payment_state` (`unallocated_php` / `is_advance` — the cash-advance list for free), `advance_php` on both balance views, both doors on one write path, and the WARN-then-RELEASE rule for deleting a receipt that has money against it. **Not built:** `rc_balance_period` (Step 6), the cheque-gap report (Step 7), reporting (Step 8).
> - **Liquidation (`/cenapro/liquidation`) — LIVE since 2026-08-05.** The UI half of Steps 2 and 3, per §7a. Four screens: the **supplier balances** table (both levels — a parent renders as a group row with its sub-suppliers nested beneath), **record/edit/void/restore a payment** (a dialog plus a per-trader payments panel), **`/cenapro/liquidation/subgroups`** (the maintenance screen for "a cheque to the parent may settle a sub-supplier's deliveries"), and **`/cenapro/liquidation/banks`** (CI's own banks + the accounts cheques are drawn on — **not optional**, since the `rc_payment` shape CHECK requires a cheque to name an account and zero accounts were seeded, so without it the dominant instrument is unrecordable). Banks and accounts are **retired with `active = false`, never deleted** — there is no delete RPC for either, and for a cheque the shape CHECK turns the FK's `ON DELETE SET NULL` into an outright refusal. The **whole route is behind `canViewPrices()` as a FETCH decision** — a denied viewer's queries are never issued, so the payload is empty because there was nothing in it. Three things it exists to get right: the sign is stated **in words** on the screen (minus = we owe them, the opposite of the accounting convention); a **non-zero balance is never an error** (decision 8 — no red, no badge); and `unpriced_receipt_count` is a **first-class column**, because `total_price_php` COALESCEs to ₱0 so a trader can read as square while carrying receipts nobody can price (SEVILLA, live). See **`liquidation/CONTEXT.md`** for the screens and "Liquidation" below for the schema. **Step 4's DATA LAYER landed 2026-08-06 but has NO UI:** there is still no allocation screen, no settlement column on the deliveries ledger and no cheque-gap report, even though `public.cenapro_rc_delivery_settlement`, `cenapro_rc_payment_state`, `cenapro_rc_payment_allocations` and the three write RPCs are live and verified. The balance views also gained `advance_php` / `advance_payment_count` / `unassigned_incoming_php` / `advance_php_window`, which no screen reads yet. **Known gap:** zero bank accounts are seeded (deliberately — Renzo types the real number), so a cheque cannot be recorded until one is added on the banks screen; the payment form links straight there. **⚠️ The 2026-08-06 opening-balance work is DATA-LAYER ONLY — there is no UI for it yet.** The balance views gained 23 / 22 columns (`opening_balance_php`, `opening_as_of_date`, `has_opening_balance`, `carried_receipt_*`, `carried_payment_*`, `*_all_php`, `unpriced_receipt_count_window`, …) that no screen reads, and no way to state a figure short of calling `public.cenapro_set_rc_supplier_opening_balance` directly. `BALANCE_COLS` / `GROUP_BALANCE_COLS` in `liquidation/actions.ts` still list the original 30 / 27 columns, so **the existing screens are unaffected and keep working** — widening them is the frontend pass. **One trap for that pass:** the comment above those constants says the projections are FULL, "so the fetched shape is exactly the generated `Row` type". That is **no longer true** for the two balance views — the projection is now a strict subset. It still typechecks only because `types/supabase.ts` has not been regenerated. **The moment someone runs `supabase gen types typescript --linked`, the `Row` aliases will carry 53 / 49 columns while `.select()` fetches 30 / 27**, and the exported `SupplierBalanceRow` / `SupplierGroupBalanceRow` aliases will over-promise. Regenerate the types and widen both constants in the same change. See "Opening balances" below.

| File | Role |
|------|------|
| `layout.tsx` | Module shell — a borderless `Card` frame (matches Production's look). No shared period provider; each screen owns its own controls. |
| `page.tsx` | Landing hub — six `hover-lift` card-links (Production / **RC Deliveries** / **Liquidation** / Flec Inventory / QC Ledger / QC Breakdown) + a "Tenant #2 · Cebu" note. |
| `liquidation/**` | **Liquidation — supplier balances, payments, subgroups (2026-08-05).** `types.ts` (PURE — the sign-in-words helpers, the unpriced stage-namer, the column table, `buildBalanceTree`, the payment vocabulary + validator), `actions.ts` (`'use server'` — the ₱ gate as a FETCH decision, plus the four write RPCs), `page.tsx` + `liquidation-view.tsx` (the balance table), `payments-panel.tsx` + `payment-dialog.tsx` (the per-trader payment list and form), `price-gate-notice.tsx`, `subgroups/{page,subgroups-view}.tsx`, and `banks/{page,banks-view}.tsx`. Full write-up in **`liquidation/CONTEXT.md`**. |
| `loading.tsx` | Table skeleton (toolbar + header + 14 row stripes). |
| `error.tsx` | Error boundary — persistent message + **Copy button** (error HARD RULE) + a note that empty data is expected pre-exposure. |
| `types.ts` | Row types **derived from the generated `types/supabase.ts`** (`cenapro_production_events` view + `cenapro_flec_balance`/`cenapro_flec_ledger` function Returns) + flec-warehouse constants + `formatDisposition()` (still used by the Flec Inventory ledger's friendly "Crusher C1" label) + the **editable-ledger lookup constants** (`SHIFT_CODES`/`GRADE_CODES`/`PLANT_CODES`/`WAREHOUSE_CODES`/`SOURCE_LOCATION_CODES`/`DISPOSITION_KINDS`/`WHSE_SIDES` + `CRUSHER_CODES`/`KILN_CODES`) + the equipment-dependency helpers `partnerEquipmentOptions()` / `dispositionRequiresEquipment()` (still consumed by `bulk-paste-utils`'s remaining validation), **and the shared CCC/FLEC single-column helpers**: `CCC_FLEC_OPTIONS` (typeahead list `FLEC`/`C1`–`C4`/`RK1`–`RK4`), **`parseCccFlec()`** (forward: one cell string → `{disposition_kind, partner_equipment_code}`, forgiving on case/aliases, null on unrecognized) and **`formatCccFlec()`** (inverse: the two DB fields → the single Excel-parity string). These two are the canonical derive/format pair shared by BOTH the inline grid's save and the bulk modal (via `bulk-paste-utils`). See "Editable-ledger lookups" + "CCC/FLEC single column" + "Data path" below. |
| `badges.ts` | **PURE module (2026-08-04)** — the tenant's display-only badge class maps: `BADGE_BASE`, `cccFlecBadgeClass` (FLEC→emerald · C1–C4→amber · RK1–RK4→rose · unknown→neutral) and `plantBadgeClass` (W6→blue · W7→teal · W6/W7→indigo · DVO→slate). Promoted VERBATIM out of `production/production-ledger-grid.tsx` when the QC Ledger's PLANT cell became a dropdown and needed the SAME plant colours: the grid is a 1,500-line client component that imports its own server actions, so borrowing two string maps from another route would have dragged all of that into the QC bundle **and into `scripts/verify-qc-draw-cells.ts`, which runs these modules under plain Node**. No React, no imports at all. `production-ledger-grid.tsx` **re-exports all three**, so the endless sheet and the mobile cards keep importing them from the ledger unchanged — one definition, four consumers, no drift. |
| `deliveries/page.tsx` | Server — **RC Deliveries**, the operators' "RC 2026" sheet as a live grid. Resolves **SCOPE** `?scope=endless\|focus` (endless default, omits the param) × **PERIOD** `?year=&month=` (focus only) × the **issue lens** `?issue=` × search `?q=`, fetches month-keys + dimensions in parallel, then either the whole month or the server-prefetched first keyset window. Keys the client by `axesKey(...)` so an axis change remounts against the new server window. Renders NO title (navbar owns titles). |
| `deliveries/types.ts` | **PURE module** — the shared vocabulary for the server page, the actions, the grid AND the verify script. Owns `stripPrices()` (the ONE ₱ boundary), the column table + `buildColumns`/`frozenOffsets`/`minTableWidth`, the **`parseSupplierCell`/`formatSupplierCell`** and **`parseDestinationCell`/`formatDestinationCell`** single-column ⇄ multi-field pairs, `weightEditText`/`priceEditText` (the formula round-trip), `sampleFieldFor` (which columns a sub-row occupies), the display formatters and the save-payload contracts. |
| `deliveries/ledger-url.ts` | **PURE module** (no `'use client'`) — `parseScope`, `resolvePeriod`/`periodBounds`/`periodLabel`, `parseIssueLens` + `ISSUE_LABELS`/`ISSUE_HINTS`, `parseQuery`, `axesKey`. Same server⇄client-shared-contract discipline as `production/ledger-url.ts`. |
| `deliveries/actions.ts` | **`'use server'`** — `fetchDeliveryPage` (bidirectional keyset pager, with NULL-date groups named explicitly in both directions so the two undated receipts stay reachable), `fetchDeliveryMonth`, `fetchDeliveryDimensions`, `fetchDeliveryMonthKeys`, `saveDeliveries`, `deleteDelivery`. Enforces the ₱ gate on every read (`canViewPrices()` → `stripPrices()` BEFORE returning) and refuses a ₱ patch key from a gated viewer. Sequences a combined field+samples save so the samples call carries the version the field patch just produced. |
| `deliveries/use-deliveries-window.ts` | Client hook — `useDeliveriesWindow(initial, lens)`, the endless sheet's self-contained bidirectional keyset pager (no TanStack Query), mirroring `production/use-ledger-window.ts`. OWNS react-virtuoso's `firstItemIndex` so a prepend and its index decrement batch together. |
| `deliveries/deliveries-ledger.tsx` | Client — the grid. **Both scopes in one component** (a `TableVirtuoso` container and a plain `<table>` container over ONE set of closures, so Tab behaves identically in each). Carries a **custom per-CELL `NavResolver`** because sample sub-rows occupy only the label lane + the seven lab lanes — `createCoordinateNavResolver`'s per-COLUMN `columnMap` cannot express that. Frozen `# · DATE · TRK# · SUPPLIER` (opaque, `.frozen-edge`, active ring at z-20), `Σ DAY TOTAL` rule-offs + a sticky month footer in focus, duplicate/flag/unmapped rails, and the WT / PHP/KG formula cells. |
| `production/page.tsx` | Server — **TWO orthogonal URL axes (a third, EDIT lock/unlock, is Phase 3):** **VIEW** `?view=ledger\|daily-w6\|daily-w7` × **SCOPE** `?scope=endless\|focus` (`endless` default + omits the param; a legacy `?focus=1` maps to `scope=focus`). Resolves the selected **period** from `?year=&batch=` (defaults to NEWEST when absent; stale/invalid → newest), then branches on BOTH axes into six no-dead-end combos: **(1) endless + ledger** → resolves the anchor (`{kind:'period'}` if the URL pair is valid, else `{kind:'latest'}`), **prefetches the FIRST keyset window server-side** (`fetchLedgerPage({mode:'anchor',anchor})`) so the initial paint is already anchored, and renders `<ProductionEndlessSheet key={anchorKey} … />` (keyed by anchor → clean remount on a dropdown jump). **(2) endless + daily-w6/w7** (Phase 2B) → resolves the SAME anchor, **prefetches the FIRST day-window server-side** (`fetchDailyPivotWindow({mode:'anchor',anchor,plant})`) so the initial paint is already anchored, and renders `<ProductionEndlessPivots key={plantView:anchorKey} … />` (keyed by plant+anchor → clean remount on a dropdown jump). The Phase-1 "coming in a later pass" fallback bar was REMOVED. **(3) focus + any view** → the month-scoped path `fetchProductionEvents(selectedPeriod)` → `<ProductionView>` (which itself switches ledger/W6/W7 on `?view=` and stays editable). Uses the pure `parseScope`/`parseViewMode`/`plantViewOf` from `ledger-url.ts`. **FILTER axis (2026-08-03):** also `parseLedgerFilters`es the six filter params SERVER-side and passes them into `fetchLedgerPage({…, filters})` so the endless prefetch is already filtered, plus down to `<ProductionEndlessSheet filters filtersActive>`; the sheet's React key becomes **`` `${anchorKey}|${filterKey}` ``** so a filter change forces the same clean remount a period jump does (fresh window + `firstItemIndex` reset by construction — no scroll jump). The daily W6/W7 pivots deliberately IGNORE the filters (they own their own plant-source filtering); the params just ride along in the URL so switching back to the ledger restores them. `searchParams` is now typed `Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>` with a local `one()` helper for the scalar params. | **`?grid=v2` (2026-08-19, temporary):** `parseGrid(params.grid)` + a `<GridVersionBar />` mounted at **every** return, so the toggle exists on all six view × scope combinations and there is no dead end. It only SWITCHES on the two LEDGER combinations — `ProductionEndlessSheetV2` (endless) and `ProductionLedgerGridV2` (focus) — because the two Daily W6/W7 pivots are merged-`rowSpan` matrices with no v2, and on those the bar's `note` says the switch changes nothing rather than letting the operator click a dead control. Both branches are handed ONE props object, so the two sides provably read the identical payload. The param is an axis of the CLIENT: it reaches no query, action or role gate.
| `production/ledger-url.ts` | **Pure URL axis helpers (NO `'use client'`)** — the single source of truth for the two enum axes, shared by the server page + the client toolbar controls without a client/server boundary hazard. Exports **VIEW** (`VIEW_MODES`/`ViewMode`/`VIEW_MODE_LABELS`/`parseViewMode` (legacy `?view=daily`→`daily-w6`)/`plantViewOf` (→`'W6'\|'W7'\|null`)) and **SCOPE** (`SCOPES`/`Scope`/`parseScope` — `endless` default + omits param, only `focus` is written). Consumed by `page.tsx` (server branching), `production-ledger-grid.tsx`, and `ledger-controls.tsx`. **FILTER axis (2026-08-03) lives here too** — the module now owns all THREE axes. Exports: `FILTER_COLUMNS` (`shift`/`grade`/`plant`/`whse`/`source`/`ccc`), `FILTER_PARAMS` (→ `?shift=&grade=&plant=&whse=&src=&ccc=`), `FILTER_NULL` (`__NULL__` sentinel for a blank cell), `LedgerFilters` (per-column `readonly string[]`; **empty array = no filter = show all**, and the param is OMITTED), `EMPTY_LEDGER_FILTERS`, `parseLedgerFilters(get)` (takes a plain getter so the SERVER page passes `(k)=>searchParams[k]` and the CLIENT passes `(k)=>useSearchParams().get(k)` — no React, no Next import), `writeLedgerFilters(sp,f)` / `deleteLedgerFilters(sp)`, `countActiveFilterColumns` / `hasActiveFilters` / `ledgerFilterKey` (order-insensitive fingerprint — the endless sheet's React key) / `describeActiveFilters` (empty-state copy), the **canonical option catalog** `FILTER_SPECS` (built from the `../types.ts` lookup constants — NOT from loaded rows) + `mergeDiscoveredOptions` / `mergeDiscoveredGroups` (append any present-but-unmapped value so nothing is unreachable) + `collectFilterPresence(rows)` (drives DIMMING of options with no rows in view), the client matcher `matchesLedgerFilters(row, filters)` over the structural `FilterableEventFields`, and **`cccFlecPredicateParts()` — the ONE place the CCC/FLEC one-column ⇄ two-DB-field split is expressed** (see "CCC/FLEC filtering" below). `actions.ts` (a `'use server'` module) imports the filter types + `cccFlecPredicateParts` from here, which is why this file must stay pure. |
| `production/ledger-controls.tsx` | Client — the shared toolbar **axis controls**: **`ViewModeSwitcher`** (segmented `?view=` control, moved here from the grid) + **`ScopeToggle`** (NEW — Endless/Focus segmented `?scope=` control, Infinity/Crosshair icons). Each writes ONE param while PRESERVING the others (view/scope/year/batch) via `useSearchParams` spread, so a scope switch keeps your view + period and a view switch keeps your scope + period; `ScopeToggle` also clears the legacy `?focus` param on any change. Rendered in BOTH the endless-sheet toolbar and the editable-grid toolbar so the axis framework reads identically across scopes. `useTransition` pending spinner. **Both accept `disabled`/`disabledHint`** (Phase 3b dirty-nav guard) — when an editor has unsaved changes the switchers dim + block navigation with a hint, matching the period picker's existing pattern. **`useLedgerFilters()` (2026-08-03)** — the client entry point to the FILTER axis, used by BOTH the focus grid and the endless sheet. Reads the six filter params, writes them with `router.replace` preserving every other param, and returns `{filters, activeCount, isPending, setColumn, clearAll}`. It keeps an **optimistic local mirror** of the pending selection (a URL write is a server round-trip; a checkbox that only ticks 300ms after the click reads as broken) — so `filters` is what the MENUS render, while the endless sheet's ROWS come from the server-applied `filters` PROP and `isPending` drives the spinner between them. Any URL change (including Back/Forward) drops the mirror, so the URL always wins. |
| `production/column-filter-menu.tsx` | Client — **the ONE multi-select filter menu** every filtered column renders, in every surface (focus grid header, endless frozen header, mobile filter sheet) so the surfaces can never drift. Built on `Popover` + `Command` (cmdk — already a dep; no new packages). **`ColumnFilterMenu`**: checkbox rows, `All`/`Clear` header actions, an optional typeahead (`searchable` — on Source + CCC/FLEC), a hover-/focus-revealed **`only`** button per row (the Excel/Sheets single-value idiom, kept as an EXPLICIT control so the primary row click stays a plain toggle), one-click **group toggles** for grouped columns ("All crushers" ⇄ "Clear Crushers"), options with no rows in the loaded window rendered **dimmed + "none here"** (never hidden, never disabled — in endless scope the value may exist further back and the query-side filter WILL find it), unmapped discovered values badged `new`, and a footer stating the current effect. Writes back in the canonical option ORDER so the URL is stable regardless of click sequence. Trigger = the same tiny chevron the old single-select used + a count chip, so the `h-8` header row does NOT grow (`showLabel={false}` on mobile, where the row already shows the column name). Also exports **`FilterSummaryChip`** (toolbar "Filters · N ✕") and **`FilteredEmptyState`** (names WHICH filters are responsible + Clear). |
| `production/actions.ts` | `'use server'` — **`fetchCenaproPeriods()`** (DISTINCT `(batch_year, batch)` from the view, deduped + sorted NEWEST-FIRST — year desc, then calendar-month desc; exports `CenaproPeriod`); **`fetchProductionEvents(period?)`** (reads `public.cenapro_production_events` for ONE period via `.eq('batch_year').eq('batch')`, newest-first by recv_date; returns `[]` when no valid period); **`fetchLedgerPage(input)`** — the **endless-sheet keyset pager** (canonical order `recv_date ASC, id ASC`; input is either an anchor (`{kind:'latest'}` DESC-then-reverse, or `{kind:'period'}` resolve first row + load forward inclusive + probe hasOlder) or a `{cursor,direction}` (`older`/`newer`) composite-keyset page via `.or('recv_date.<op>.D,and(recv_date.eq.D,id.<op>.I)')`, page size 100, returns `{rows, hasOlder, hasNewer, notice?, error?}`; reuses the EXACT single-string-literal `.select(...)` as `fetchProductionEvents`; exports `LedgerAnchor`/`LedgerCursor`/`LedgerPageInput`/`LedgerPage`). **Both input modes now carry an optional `filters: LedgerFilters`, applied by the module-local `applyLedgerFilters()` to EVERY branch — anchor-latest, the period anchor's first-row resolution, the period forward load, the `hasOlder` count probe, cursor-older and cursor-newer** — see "Endless filtering is a QUERY, not a view" below); **`fetchDailyPivotWindow(input)`** — the **endless W6/W7 DAY-windowed keyset pager** (Phase 2B). Paginates by WHOLE `prod_date`s (never a partial day — a page = the next `DAILY_WINDOW_DAYS`=12 COMPLETE days). Input is an anchor (`{kind:'latest'}` newest N days, or `{kind:'period'}` = the period's first plant-source prod_date then forward + probe hasOlder) or a `{cursor:prod_date, direction:'older'|'newer'}` day-cursor. **Filters the plant source set (`SOURCE_SETS[plant]`, FLEC/DVO excluded) SERVER-SIDE** so a window never ships useless rows AND every returned distinct `prod_date` yields exactly one day-block (day counting for the client `firstItemIndex` stays exact). Two-step: distinct prod_dates (dedupe an ordered single-column read) → all events for those dates (`.in('prod_date', dates)`, oldest-first `prod_date/recv_date/id`). Returns `{events, hasOlder, hasNewer, notice?, error?}`; exports `DailyPivotWindowInput`/`DailyPivotWindow`; imports `SOURCE_SETS`/`PlantView` from `production-sources.ts`; **and `saveProductionEvents(dirtyRows, deletedIds)`** (upsert + delete through the auto-updatable view; coerces strings→number/date, strips empties→null, NEVER sends `unique_tag`; `revalidatePath('/cenapro/production')` — re-fetches the current URL period). Exports `ProductionEventDirtyRow` — which now carries an **OPTIONAL `batch_year?: string`**: normally omitted (the base trigger derives `batch_year` from `YEAR(recv_date)`), but the endless pivots send it explicitly per new pull / re-tag (= the pull's own prod_date year, or the original event's year on a re-tag) so a cross-year pull lands in its OWN period; the coercion sends it only when parseable. |
| `production/production-endless-sheet.tsx` | Client — **the Endless Sheet (Phase 1, READ-ONLY)**: ONE continuous, virtualized view of the ENTIRE `cenapro_production_events` history, **oldest-first** (`recv_date ASC, id ASC`), lazy-loaded bidirectionally. Built on **`react-virtuoso`'s `TableVirtuoso`** (new dependency) — chosen for its **`firstItemIndex`** prepend-anchor mechanism (the hook decrements it by the prepended count IN THE SAME state batch as the row prepend → viewport stays pinned, no scroll jump, while older history streams in above). `startReached`→`fetchOlder`, `endReached`→`fetchNewer`; `initialTopMostItemIndex = rows.length-1` for `latest` (opens at the BOTTOM = newest), `0` for a period (opens at its first row). **Columns/widths/formatting MIRROR the ledger** (same colgroup `36/96/96/120/64/80/84/108/84/104/112/72/72`, min-width 1228, 12 data cols `# · recv · prod · batch · shift · grade · plant · whse · source · weight · CCC/FLEC · flec · side`) by REUSING the ledger's **exported** display helpers (`toGridRow`/`rowDirection`/`rowDirectionTint`/`rowDirectionFrozenTint`/`cccFlecBadgeClass`/`plantBadgeClass`/`BADGE_BASE`/`formatKg`) + `formatDateShort` from `@/components/shared/grid` — read-only cells, NO edit/selection. **Frozen 4 identity columns** (`.frozen-col`/`.frozen-corner`/`.frozen-edge`, cumulative left `0/36/132/228`) + **opaque frozen header** via `fixedHeaderContent` (custom `TableHead` = `frozen-row bg-muted`, z-index bumped to 20 so it out-ranks the z-10 frozen body cols). Custom `Scroller` adds `overflow-x:auto` (never-crush). **Month separators** are 1:1 with data rows (NO separator items → zero `firstItemIndex` drift): a boundary is derived inside `itemContent` by comparing a row to its predecessor, then rendered AS PART of that row — a `border-t-2 border-t-primary/40` across the row + a slim month label stacked in the always-visible frozen Recv cell. **NO sort/filter controls** (locked decision — those stay in Focus scope). Thin absolute "Loading earlier/newer entries…" affordances at the edges (NO row animations). **Toolbar = `CenaproPeriodPicker` + the shared `ViewModeSwitcher` (mode="ledger") + the shared `ScopeToggle` (scope="endless")** from `ledger-controls.tsx` (the old local `LedgerModeToggle` was removed) — so a View or Scope switch here `router.replace`s the URL and the server re-routes to the matching combo. loadError → persistent copyable banner (HARD RULE). Mobile: desktop-first — horizontal-scrolls (never-crush min-width) on phones, doesn't crash; the full phone card experience stays in Focus scope's `MobileCardList`. **DRAFT ENTRY ZONE (Phase 2A):** a toolbar **lock/unlock toggle** (UI-only state, NOT URL — "Add rows"/"Unlocked"); locked (default) = the read-only sheet bottoms out at the newest committed row, unlocked = a maintained pool of IN-LIST BLANK draft rows is appended below the last committed row IN THE SAME virtuoso list (you scroll DOWN into an effectively-infinite supply). **Blank-pool growth = TWO orthogonal mechanisms (refined 2026-07-22):** (1) **minimum-buffer maintenance** (`ensureBlankBuffer`, on the draftRows effect + on paste) keeps ~25 trailing blanks past the last non-blank draft (so there are always blanks before any scroll, and an Excel paste taller than the pool auto-extends) — idempotent, no-op once full; (2) **scroll-growth** — every genuine bottom-reach appends a fresh `BLANK_GROW_BATCH` (25) of blanks UNCONDITIONALLY (decoupled from typed content), so scrolling down ALWAYS reveals more rows with no visible cap. Scroll-growth is driven by **`atBottomStateChange`, NOT `endReached`** (the old `endReached`+buffer logic hit a hard floor at ~25 rows because trailing already ≥ trigger → never grew; and with a bottom overscan `endReached` re-fires as appended blanks settle → would runaway). **Anti-runaway guard:** a `growGuardRef` set on append, cleared only when the operator scrolls back UP off the bottom (`atBottom → false`); since `atBottomStateChange` fires only on a true↔false TRANSITION and one append (25 rows ≈ taller than the overscan) shoves the end far below the viewport → `atBottom` flips false → guard resets, one bottom-reach = exactly one append, and the next batch needs a fresh scroll-down (no append → re-render → re-append loop). `endReached` now only pages newer COMMITTED history via `fetchNewer` (a no-op at the true-latest drafting edge). **Save/Discard controls (relocated 2026-07-22):** live IN THE TOOLBAR beside the "Add rows"/"Unlocked" toggle — a primary **"Save N rows"** + a ghost **"Discard all"** (→ `AlertDialog` confirm, the ONE destructive action) — shown only when `unlocked && draftCount > 0`. The old floating glass Save bar at the bottom was REMOVED (the toolbar is now the single home for these controls). **Chrome-only motion polish (2026-07-22, CLAUDE.md hard rule — ZERO row/draft-row animation):** the Save/Discard group `animate-scale-in`s on unlock; the Save button's draft count `animate-badge-pop`s when it changes (keyed `<span>`); an unlocked "N ready"/"Type or paste below" hint `animate-fade-in`s; a post-save "Saved N rows" toolbar cue `animate-fade-up`s then auto-clears (2.5s); the toggle + buttons carry `transition-colors duration-150` hover/state polish. All motion is on toolbar chrome — the virtualized committed rows AND the draft rows keep only the pre-existing `transition-colors` hover (allowed) and get no entrance/stagger/fade (they recycle). Draft rows live HERE (state) and are **mirrored to `localStorage`** (`cenapro-ledger-drafts:v1:<user-id>`, debounced 300ms, restored on mount, cleared ONLY on confirmed Save/Discard) so nothing can destroy them (the modal lost ~20 rows repeatedly — BUG-012). **Single "Add rows" button (one action):** clicking it — if NOT at the true bottom (`hasNewer === true`, e.g. you jumped to an old month) first `reset({kind:'latest'})`s + scrolls to bottom, THEN reveals blanks; if already at the bottom just reveals blanks — either way you end unlocked at the true append edge (drafts NEVER append to a mid-history window). No separate "jump to latest" affordance. Draft data lives in the parent-owned `draftRows` array (keyed by position) rendered through virtuoso `itemContent` — recycling-safe; `firstItemIndex` (top prepend) and the bottom blank-append are orthogonal. Save validates via `mapBulkRowToDirty` (persistent copyable `errorToast` + red rails on error), commits via the EXISTING `saveProductionEvents(dirtyRows, [])`, then calls the hook's **`refreshNewest()`** so saved rows appear at the bottom without a full reload + `scrollToIndex('LAST')`. A one-shot `?add=1` URL intent (from the focus toolbar's "Add rows in the sheet →" affordance) auto-unlocks on arrival then strips itself. **RESTORE IS EXPLICIT + CONSENTED (2026-07-22, no silent auto-unlock):** on reload with ≥1 non-blank draft in localStorage the sheet loads in its NORMAL LOCKED read-only state (drafts are restored into `draftRows` state but stay DORMANT — blanks/drafts only render when unlocked), and a small non-blocking inline **Resume/Discard prompt** (`resumePrompt` state, an `animate-fade-up` `bg-card` banner under the toolbar — NOT a modal, NOT a toast) shows **"You have N unsaved draft row(s) from a previous session."** (N = non-blank restored count). **Resume** → `setUnlocked(true)` + scroll to the append edge (via `pendingScrollBottomRef`) + dismiss, dropping the operator back into editing with drafts intact; **Discard** → clear drafts (`setDraftRows([])` + explicit `clearDraftMirror()` → the mirror else-branch also `removeItem`s the key) + stay locked + dismiss; an **✕** = "later" (keep drafts, stay locked). The `?add=1` intent is REMEMBERED on the prompt (`wantAdd`): `?add=1` + NO drafts → unlock directly (no prompt); `?add=1` + drafts → still show the prompt, and there Discard clears the stale drafts AND unlocks fresh for adding (so an explicit add-intent never dead-ends). `useAuth()` supplies the user-id key. **INLINE EDIT OF COMMITTED ROWS (Phase 3a):** under the SAME unlock, committed rows become inline-editable — the endless sheet is now ONE unified editing surface (drafts + committed) over ONE coordinate space (`totalRows = committed.length + draftRows.length`; committed rows occupy coords `[0..C-1]`, drafts `C+draftIndex`, which equals the row's data-index into virtuoso's `items`). The shared grid hooks (`useCellSelection`/`useGridKeyboardNav`/`useGridEditSession` + a custom region-routing paste) address BOTH regions; `getCellValue`/`updateRow`/`removeRow`/`commitDateCell` route by the region boundary `C`. **Recycle-/prepend-safe pending state (non-negotiable):** modified committed cells live in a PARENT-OWNED `editedRows: Map<eventId, BulkRow>` (the FULL merged row per id, so an edit is self-contained + saveable even for an off-window / not-loaded row) and deleted committed rows in `deletedIds: Set<eventId>` — BOTH keyed by the stable event `id`, NEVER in a recycled virtual row's local state, so a row edited → scrolled off → back rehydrates its pending value from the map by id (prepending older pages shifts array indices but not ids). Committed rows render editable via `CommittedRowCells` (draft-entry-zone) reusing the SAME `EditableDataCells` the drafts use, so unlocked committed + draft rows are visually/behaviorally identical (a flat, non-frozen spreadsheet); LOCKED committed rows stay byte-for-byte the read-only frozen `renderCommittedCells` (dormant restored edits do NOT paint until unlocked). A per-row **Delete/Restore** control lives in the committed row's # cell (hover-revealed trash → strike-through + rose rail; flips to Restore) — chosen over a right-click menu to match the drafts' hover-× and avoid wiring a context menu into the virtualized list. **Unified dirty model + ONE Save bar:** counts span new + modified + deleted (`totalDirty`, compact "3 new · 2 mod · 1 del" label); Save builds ONE payload — drafts (no id → INSERT) + modified committed (with id → UPDATE, built from the `editedRows` map directly) + `deletedIds` — through the EXISTING `saveProductionEvents(dirtyRows, deletedIds)` (validation via `mapBulkRowToDirty` for both; error keys `d:<draftIndex>` / `c:<id>` → red rails + persistent copyable `errorToast`); Discard clears all three (+ mirror). **Post-save refresh:** drafts always land at the newest end → `win.reset(anchor)` + scroll to the bottom entry edge; a committed-ONLY change → the new **`win.refreshWindow()`** refetches the loaded window in place + re-anchors the viewport near the pre-save top row id (a `recv_date` edit may relocate the row in canonical order — accepted; the fresh server read never crashes on a relocation). **localStorage mirror is now v2** (`cenapro-ledger-drafts:v2:<user-id>`, shape `{ drafts, edits, deletes }`) — covers pending edits + deletes too; the Resume/Discard reload prompt counts new + modified + deleted. |
| `production/use-ledger-window.ts` | Client hook — **`useLedgerWindow(initialPage, filters?)`**: the endless sheet's self-contained bidirectional keyset pager (NO TanStack Query). Seeds from the server-prefetched `initialPage`; holds the flat `rows` window + `firstItemIndex` (OWNED here so the prepend + index decrement batch together), `hasOlder`/`hasNewer`, `loadingOlder`/`loadingNewer`, `notice`; exposes `fetchOlder()` (prepends the page before the window's first row + decrements `firstItemIndex`), `fetchNewer()` (appends after the last row), and `reset(anchor)` (drops + re-seeds). Cursors derive from the first/last row of the current window; per-direction guards (refs) ignore concurrent/exhausted calls; de-dups by `id` defensively. Calls the `fetchLedgerPage` server action directly; errors → `errorToast`. **The active `filters` are held in a ref and passed to EVERY `fetchLedgerPage` call** (older / newer / reset / refreshNewest / refreshWindow) — miss one and the keyset walk silently drifts back to unfiltered history. **`refreshNewest()` (Phase 2A):** walks forward from the current tail (local cursor, past the `hasNewer` guard, bounded loop, de-duped) appending every row newer than the window — called AFTER a draft Save so freshly-committed rows land at the bottom without a full reload (`revalidatePath` refreshes only the server tree, not this in-memory pager); re-seeds from `latest` if the window is empty. **`refreshWindow()` (Phase 3a):** re-pulls the CURRENTLY-loaded window fresh from the server after a Save that touched COMMITTED rows (inline edits / deletes can live on ANY loaded page, and a `recv_date` edit can relocate a row in canonical order, so append-only `refreshNewest` isn't enough) — re-seeds from the current top row's period anchor (falls back to `latest`), then re-paginates forward (bounded loop) to restore the prior loaded depth, keeping the viewport near where the operator was editing. Crash-safe: a fresh server read, never an in-memory merge of stale overlays, so a relocated/vanished row just lands in its new spot (or falls outside the window). |
| `production/period-picker.tsx` | Client — **local** Year + Batch picker (`CenaproPeriodPicker`) for the ledger toolbar, mirroring the ICTC production picker but URL-driven (no shared cross-tab context — Cenapro is a single page). Changing either select `router.replace`s `?year=&batch=` inside a `useTransition` (shows a spinner) → server re-fetch of that period. **Preserves the other axis params (`view`/`scope`)** so a period jump keeps the current view + scope (jump-to anchor in endless, clamp selector in focus — the same control). Year change snaps to that year's newest batch if the current batch is absent. **No "All periods" option** (that's the slow path this removes). Disabled (with a hint title) while the grid has unsaved edits. |
| `production/production-view.tsx` | Client — thin wrapper: `router.refresh()` on save success, and keys `<ProductionLedgerGrid>` by a fingerprint that folds in the **selected period** + the server-row ids, so a successful save OR a period switch remounts against the scoped/saved truth (fresh ids + computed `unique_tag`/`batch_year`, dirty state cleared). |
| `production/production-ledger-grid.tsx` | Client — the **editable** dense Excel-style ledger (one VIEW row = one grid row, keyed by stable `id`), scoped to one period. **12 data columns** (Excel order): `# · recv · prod · batch · shift · grade · plant · whse · source · weight · CCC/FLEC · flec · side`. Inline editing via shared `GridCell` + cell hooks (`useCellSelection`/`useClipboardCopy`/`useCellDelete`); `DatePickerCell` for recv/prod dates; **`SelectCell`** (a DropdownMenu cell) for the categorical columns (shift/grade/plant/whse/source/side); numeric `<Input>` for weight/flec; and the **single `CCC/FLEC` typeahead cell** — a paste-friendly `GridCell` + `<Input list=ledger-ccc-flec-suggestions>` (NOT a dropdown) holding the raw Excel value (`FLEC`/`Cn`/`RKn`). That merged cell is stored as a raw-text `ccc_flec` field on the grid row (seeded from the two DB fields via `formatCccFlec` on load, edited directly so typing isn't fought by a derive) and **parsed back into `disposition_kind` + `partner_equipment_code` at SAVE via the shared `parseCccFlec`** — an unrecognized value is rejected with a persistent `errorToast` (HARD RULE) naming the row(s); a single typed cell can't make an inconsistent pair, so the old equipment-dependency dance / separate Disposition+Equipment columns are gone. **Rows are rendered via a `React.memo`'d `ProductionRow`** fed PRIMITIVE slices of the global active/selection state + stable `useCallback` handlers, so editing/selecting one cell re-renders only the affected rows. Dirty tracking (`existing`/`new`/`modified`/`deleted` with amber/blue left borders), **NO trailing always-empty new row** (adds happen via the right-click Insert Above/Below context menu, or via the endless sheet's draft entry zone — the retired Bulk Add modal's replacement; paste-into-the-grid may still auto-extend rows), paste-from-Excel (TSV — `ccc_flec` lands as raw text), right-click context menu (Insert Above/Below, Duplicate, Delete/Restore), single Save/Discard. **The 4 leftmost identity columns (`# · Recv · Prod · Batch`) are FROZEN (Excel freeze panes)** — sticky-left with cumulative px offsets (left: 0 / 36 / 132 / 228 from the colgroup widths 36/96/96/120), so they stay pinned when scrolling right through the wide grid; the last frozen column (Batch) carries the canonical `.frozen-edge` anti-seam right divider. Frozen surfaces follow the canonical Frozen Panes pattern (CLAUDE.md + globals.css) — **fully OPAQUE**, never glass: header/corner `bg-muted` solid (`.frozen-corner`), body `bg-background` base + the row's opaque IN/OUT tint (`.frozen-col`), so scrolling content can't bleed THROUGH the pinned cells; z-scale stacks frozen header corners (z-30) > scrolling header row (z-20) > frozen body col (z-10) > normal cells. The empty state shows when the selected period has **zero data rows**. **Header filters = Shift / Grade / Plant / Warehouse / Source** (options distinct-in-data; Disposition filter removed). **Both the Recv and Prod date headers are clickable to sort** (`DateSortHeader` — click a date header to make it the sort key, click again to flip asc/desc; the up/down chevron marks the active key, an idle `ChevronsUpDown` the other) — in-memory, default newest-first by recv_date; the old toolbar "Date" button is gone. Toolbar hosts the **`CenaproPeriodPicker`** (left) + an **"Add rows in the sheet →" affordance** (`goAddInSheet`) that replaced the old "Bulk Add" button — it `router.push`es to endless+ledger dropping `year`/`batch` (→ `latest` anchor) with a one-shot `?add=1` so the endless sheet opens UNLOCKED at the true-latest append edge with the loss-proof draft zone ready; disabled while the grid `isDirty`. `SelectCell` + `DatePickerCell` are now IMPORTED from `@/components/shared/grid` (Phase 2 of the Blackwood Table consolidation — the local copies were promoted to the shared barrel in Phase 0 and deleted here; byte-identical, no drift). Still-local: `ColumnFilterMenu` + `DateSortHeader` + `ProductionRow`. **Cell selection / inline edit / keyboard nav / paste** run on the shared Blackwood Table hooks — `useGridKeyboardNav` (coordinate resolver wrapped to clamp ArrowLeft→col 1 and to gate inline editing to typable columns via a custom `isEditable`; Home/End handled in a container wrapper), `useGridEditSession` (with a CUSTOM revert that un-dirties a reverted modified-existing row), and the existing `useCellSelection`/`useClipboardCopy`/`useCellDelete` wired into the hook's `range` slot; `enableEnterAnchor: false` (plain Enter drops down). The grid keeps its OWN `handleSmartPaste` (paste must flip pasted existing rows to `modified`). The right-click row context menu now uses the shared `useGridContextMenu` hook + declarative `GridContextMenu` component (Phase 4 of the Blackwood Table consolidation — viewport edge-flip + close-on-outside/Esc + menu styling all lifted into the shared primitives; see `components/shared/grid/CONTEXT.md`). Items are a `GridMenuItem<number>[]` keyed on `rowIdx` (Insert Above/Below, Duplicate, separator, Delete/Restore); Delete (destructive) and Restore (muted) are TWO items gated by `hidden` on the row's deleted state, since the shared `variant` is static. **Visual styling (display-only):** the whole ledger is **bold** (`font-bold` on headers, data cells, and the edit `<Input>` so weight doesn't shift on F2). Each row carries a **bold row tint** keyed on the WAREHOUSE first, then the CCC/FLEC disposition — **priority: UNPLACED > WHSE 3 (DVO) > disposition** (via `rowDirection(row)`, which reads `warehouse_code` + the shared `parseCccFlec` — same source as save, so it re-tints live as you place/type): UNPLACED (warehouse blank) → no tint (an unplaced bagging row is no longer green until placed); WHSE 3 (DVO) → blue (`bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-950/40`) regardless of disposition; a real placed warehouse (WHSE 1/2/5/7) → bagged-IN (`flec_bagging`) → `bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/40`, any withdrawal (`partner_crusher`/`partner_kiln`) → `bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/40`; unrecognized at a real warehouse → no tint. (Two tint helpers: `rowDirectionTint` = the translucent scrolling-`<tr>` flavour above; `rowDirectionFrozenTint` = the OPAQUE flavour for the sticky frozen identity cells — `dark:` bumps to `-950/60`, blue included, so the pinned cells aren't see-through.) The tint is applied FIRST in the `<tr>` class so the dirty-state left borders (new=blue / modified=amber / deleted=strikethrough) + hover + cell-selection highlight all read on top. **Two columns render colored badges in display mode** (helpers `cccFlecBadgeClass` / `plantBadgeClass` + shared `BADGE_BASE` — **defined in the pure `../badges.ts` since 2026-08-04 and RE-EXPORTED from here**, so every existing import site is unchanged while the QC Ledger's PLANT dropdown can share the exact same colours without importing this file across a route boundary; the edit input stays a plain `<Input>` so typing/paste is unaffected) — punchier than before (`/20`–`/25` fill, `/40` border, bold `text-{c}-700 dark:text-{c}-300`): **CCC/FLEC** — `FLEC`→emerald, `C1`–`C4` (crushers)→amber, `RK1`–`RK4` (kilns)→rose, unrecognized→neutral; **PLANT** — `W6`→blue, `W7`→teal, `W6/W7`→indigo, `DVO`→slate, empty→plain (rendered via a `SelectCell` `renderTrigger` prop that styles ONLY the closed trigger; dropdown menu items stay plain text). Every badge color has a `dark:` text variant. No row animations (CLAUDE.md hard rule). **Axis toolbar:** the toolbar carries the shared **`ViewModeSwitcher`** (3-mode `?view=` segmented control) + the shared **`ScopeToggle`** (Endless/Focus `?scope=` control) — BOTH imported from `ledger-controls.tsx` (the grid's local `ViewModeSwitcher` was moved there in the Phase-1 reframe); the `ViewMode`/`parseViewMode`/`plantViewOf` helpers are imported from the pure `ledger-url.ts`. The grid reads its own `currentScope` from the URL (`?focus=1`→focus, else `parseScope('scope')`) and passes it to `ScopeToggle` so the toggle mirrors the real scope even in the endless+daily fallback. `?view=` legacy `daily`→`daily-w6`; `?view` defaults to `ledger`. When a daily mode is active: the period picker + switcher STAY visible but the editable-only controls (row count, Clear-filters, "Add rows in the sheet", Discard, Save) are HIDDEN and `<ProductionDailyBlock rows={initialRows} plantView={…} />` renders in place of the editable `<table>` (plus its context menu / datalist); the `loadError` banner still shows. The grid's hooks always run (only the JSX branches), and Ledger mode behavior is unchanged. **Mobile (Archetype C):** in Ledger mode the desktop `<table>` container is `hidden sm:block` and a `sm:hidden` **`<CenaproLedgerCardsMobile>`** renders instead on phones, fed a `mobileRows` memo (`rows` minus deleted/empty-new/filter-hidden — single source of truth) + the shared filter state/options/setters; the display-only badge helpers (`cccFlecBadgeClass`/`plantBadgeClass`/`BADGE_BASE`/`formatKg`) and the `GridRow` type are now **`export`ed** for that card file to reuse. The **toolbar is `flex-wrap`** so the period picker + view switcher + controls don't overflow at 375px (helps every view mode). See `production-ledger-cards-mobile.tsx`. |
| `production/production-ledger-cards-mobile.tsx` | Client — the **phone read layer for the LEDGER** (`?view=ledger` only; the daily-w6/w7 matrix is a later phase). Built on the platform **`MobileCardList<GridRow>`** primitive (**Archetype C** — dense desktop table → phone card list; see `components/shared/mobile/CONTEXT.md`). **ADDITIVE + read-only** — the whole editable layer (inline edit, Bulk Add, paste, context menu) stays desktop-only (click-to-select cells degrade on touch). The grid renders the desktop `<table>` `hidden sm:block` and this card list `sm:hidden`, fed the **SAME sorted/filtered rows** (`mobileRows` = the ledger's `rows` minus deleted/empty-new/filter-hidden — single source of truth, no refetch). **Card headline (≤6):** `recv · batch · shift/grade · source · weight` + a **CCC/FLEC disposition badge** and a **Plant chip** — status meaning is preserved via the SAME desktop badge helpers (`cccFlecBadgeClass` / `plantBadgeClass` over the shared `BADGE_BASE`, all now **exported** from `production-ledger-grid.tsx`, plus `formatKg` + the `GridRow` type — reused, not duplicated, mirroring how RC IN's `delivery-cards-mobile` imports from its sibling desktop table), NOT a bare row tint. **Detail bottom-Sheet:** every remaining field (prod date, batch_year, warehouse, side, flec count, weight, CCC/FLEC). **Mobile Filters Sheet** (2026-08-03: now MULTI-select — it renders the shared `ColumnFilterMenu` per column, driven by the SAME URL filter axis, with a per-row selection summary; props collapsed from 17 single-select fields to `filters`/`presence`/`activeFilterCount`/`setFilterColumn`/`clearFilters`) formerly reused the desktop single-select column filters (Shift/Grade/Plant/Whse/Source, `'ALL'`=off) via the SAME state + options + setters threaded from the grid (so mobile ⇄ desktop filter sets never diverge). **"View full table" escape hatch** mounts a read-only mirror of the ledger columns in a horizontally-scrolling Sheet (NOT the interactive grid). No ₱ in the PRODUCTION ledger → nothing to gate on this screen. (Cenapro as a whole is no longer ₱-free: **RC Deliveries introduced money to this tenant on 2026-08-04** and IS price-gated — see "Price gating" below.) |
| `production/production-daily-block.tsx` | Client — the **EDITABLE Daily Block** view (Phase 1 + 2), a visually-styled rebuild of the boss's "PROD 2026" spreadsheet, rendered as **two plant variants** (`plantView: 'W6' \| 'W7'` prop — Daily W6 / Daily W7 view modes). **PROD-ONLY** — always groups by `prod_date` (the recv/prod toggle was removed; this is a production view by definition). Consumes the SAME period rows the ledger holds (`ProductionEventRow[]`). **Source filter (production OUTPUT only):** rows are filtered by `source_location_code` against `SOURCE_SETS[plantView]` before pivoting — **W6 = `{TNK 1, TNK 2, TNK 3, TNK 4, W6}`, W7 = `{W7}`**; **FLEC and DVO are EXCLUDED from BOTH** (inventory withdrawals, not factory output). `buildDateGroups(rows, plantView)` (pure) pivots into **PROD_DATE → SHIFT (M/E/N) → GRADE (3X50/2X6/3.5/4X8) → SOURCE → RECV_DATE** — **a LEAF row = one (source, recv_date) pull** (all events matching that 5-tuple aggregated into the equipment columns). The **SOURCE cell `rowSpan`s its recv-date rows**, so a source appears ONCE with its recv dates stacked **chronologically** beside it in a new **Recv Date** column (you can see how many pulls a tank had that prod date). Columns `Date · Shift · Grade · Source · Recv Date · C1–C4 · RK1–RK4 · Bagging · Sub-Total · Total`: equipment col = SUM(`weight_kg`) where `partner_equipment_code` = that code; **Bagging KEPT** = SUM where `disposition_kind='flec_bagging'`; **Sub-Total** = Σ partner; **Total** = Sub-Total + Bagging. **Only rollup = the per-DATE "Daily total" footer** (the grade subtotal row + all subtotal logic were REMOVED; a grade just lists its source→recv rows). **FIXED equipment columns (not adaptive):** all 8 (C1–C4, RK1–RK4) always render; blank cells blank (`0 → ''`). **Padded + outlined day blocks:** each day is padded with blank FILLER rows up to `MIN_DAY_ROWS` (=6, **tunable**) — filler rows sit after the last real row, before the footer; their numeric cells get a faint `bg-muted/30` so the area reads as a reserved/empty SLOT (not missing data), still on the uniform grid + inside the day box. **Excel-matched widths + density:** colgroup px (Date 76 / Shift 48 / Grade 100 / Source 140 / **Recv Date 80** / each crusher+kiln 56 / Bagging 66 / Sub-Total 70 / Total 84), table `width: max-content`, compact `px-1.5 py-0.5` / `text-[11px]`. **Complete 2-tier header (one solid bordered band edge-to-edge):** the **5 IDENTITY headers `rowSpan={2}`** so each fills the FULL header height (no empty white top-left), vertically centered, neutral muted bg, `.frozen-corner` (sticky top AND left, highest z, opaque) — labels are `Date · SHFT · Grade · Source · Recv Date` (the Shift header reads **`SHFT`**, not `Sh`; the real-row merged shift cell stays the big colored letter); the numeric side has the tinted group banner (Crushers/Kilns/Bagging/Totals) over the per-column code cells. **Layout — REAL merged cells via `rowSpan`:** DATE `rowSpan`s the day's REAL + FILLER rows (**NOT** the footer); SHIFT `rowSpan`s its shift's rows; GRADE `rowSpan`s its grade's leaf rows (`= leafCount`); SOURCE `rowSpan`s its recv-date rows. `dateRowSpan = dayBody + fillerCount`; the day has `dayBody + fillerCount + 1` `<tr>`s (the +1 footer is a separate full-width row) — **rectangular**. Merged identity content is **align-top** (label sits atop its block). Merged cells sticky-left (frozen) + OPAQUE. **THREE border weights, one token each (used everywhere — header, body, filler, footer):** **GRID** = uniform 1px `border-b border-r border-border` (solid, crisp) on EVERY cell, both axes (merged cells skip internal horizontal lines = intended merge look); **GROUP** = a 2px `border-l-2 border-l-border` divider between the column groups (Crushers | Kilns | Bagging | Totals), carried through header + body + filler + footer; **BOX** = the 2px day-block outline on the day's edge cells (`border-t-2` first row, `border-b-2` footer, `border-l-2` Date col, `border-r-2` Total col → survives horizontal scroll). **SOFT + NEUTRAL** — `border-border` (the SAME color as the gridlines, just 2px thick); the day box stands out by WEIGHT, not color (NOT `border-foreground`/black, which read as a harsh black outline on the perimeter cells). The whole table's outer perimeter is closed with a `border border-border` on the `<table>`. Clean card background, **no day tint**. **BOLD identity columns:** Date/Shift/Grade/Source/Recv Date cells render bold; numeric cells stay normal weight (the daily-total row is bold). **Frozen panes** — **5 identity columns** (Date/Shift/Grade/Source/**Recv Date**) + the 2-tier header, cumulative `left` offsets, `.frozen-edge` on **Recv Date**. **Full-width day footer:** a TRUE edge-to-edge totals row — a "Daily total" label cell `colSpan={5}` over ALL FIVE identity columns (sticky at `left:0`), then the equipment/bag/sub/total cells; ONE continuous solid `bg-muted` band (opaque across frozen cols), bold numbers, the box top + bottom edge + the group dividers. **Visual flair:** **shift = a BOLD colored letter** M amber / E violet / N indigo (NOT a filled pill — cannot overflow the 48px column); per-grade **chips** (3X50 sky / 2X6 teal / 3.5 amber / 4X8 rose); the group banner tints (Crushers amber | Kilns rose | Bagging emerald | Totals neutral) sit on the GROUP cells only. **No data-bars** (numbers render plain). Single container `animate-fade-in`, **NO per-row animation**. Empty state per variant ("No W6/W7 production output for this period"). **EDITABLE (Phase 1) — equipment/bagging NUMBERS on real pull rows, round-trip via the EXISTING `saveProductionEvents(dirtyRows, deletedIds)` (NO new backend):** the 8 equipment cols (C1–C4, RK1–RK4) + Bagging on REAL leaf rows are editable via the **canonical Blackwood Table click-to-select / type-to-edit model** (NOT always-on inputs anymore — see KEYBOARD NAVIGATION below): each renders a focusable STATIC cell by default and mounts the shared `EditInput` only while editing; **commit is EXPLICIT (registry-driven), NOT blur-driven** (see EXPLICIT-COMMIT below), Esc reverts; real-row identity cells + the footer stay read-only `<span>`s. Three ops: **EDIT** a value → UPDATE (dirty row WITH the cell's underlying event `id`, amber ring); **CLEAR** a value → DELETE (id → `deletedIds`, guarded by an `AlertDialog` confirm); **TYPE into a blank cell** → a **COLUMN-AWARE `Popover`** → stage an INSERT (dirty row WITHOUT id, blue tint). The popover always shows read-only **CONTEXT chips** (auto Plant W6/W7, prod date, shift, grade, target column e.g. "C3 · Crusher" / "Bagging") and branches on the column: **Crushers/Kilns (C1–C4, RK1–RK4)** → the only field is **Source** (a selector over `SOURCE_SETS[plantView]`, pre-filled to the row's source) — warehouse/side/flec are dropped (noise here, left null); **Bagging** → **Source + Warehouse** (`WAREHOUSE_CODES`, "— Unplaced"=null) + **Side** (`LS`/`RS`, nullable) + **Flec count** (optional). **Cell→event mapping:** the pivot records each cell's contributing event `id`(s) (`CellSlot.eventIds`) so UPDATE/DELETE target the exact event; the **COLUMN is the disposition** via `columnDisposition()` (C1–C4→`partner_crusher`+code; RK1–RK4→`partner_kiln`+code; Bagging→`flec_bagging`+null — same map as `parseCccFlec`/`partnerEquipmentOptions`, CHECK structurally satisfied). **INSERT dims** come from the leaf (recv_date/prod_date/shift/grade) + the popover's **Source** (defaults to the row's source) + `selectedPeriod.batch`, and **`plant_code` is AUTO-STAMPED = `plantView` (W6/W7)** so a new entry never shows a blank Plant in the Ledger; `batch_year` is NOT sent (the base trigger derives it from recv_date — correct for same-year periods; `ProductionEventDirtyRow` has no `batch_year` field, so the contract's cross-year override isn't expressible Phase 1 and is deferred). **COLLISION LOCK:** a cell whose 9-col pivot key maps to >1 event renders read-only with a 🔒 + "Multiple entries — edit in Ledger" (exactly 1 such cell in live W6/W7 data: MAY-2026 / TNK 2 / 3X50 bagging). **State + save:** `modified` (eventId→weight), `staged` (cellKey→INSERT dirty row), `deleted` (event ids); a Save/Discard bar (with a "N unsaved" count) appears in the toolbar when `hasChanges`; **Save** calls `saveProductionEvents(dirtyRows, deletedIds)` then `onSaveSuccess()` (→ the production-view `dataKey` remount / router.refresh); errors via `errorToast` (HARD RULE). Props `selectedPeriod` + `onSaveSuccess` are threaded from `production-ledger-grid.tsx`'s daily-block render. **EDITABLE (Phase 2) — NEW PULLS + NEW DAYS (same staged-INSERT → `saveProductionEvents` path):** the blank FILLER rows are **"new pull" input slots** (rendered FLAT, not merged): the identity cells are **Excel-style live typeahead** (`IdentitySuggestInput`, NOT a `<Select>` and NOT a native `<datalist>`) — **Shift** (`SHIFT_CODES`), **Grade** (`GRADE_CODES`), **Source** (`SOURCE_SETS[plantView]`). **Why a custom dropdown, not `<datalist>` (Issue 2a):** native `<datalist>` is unreliable for live typeahead — most notably it shows NOTHING for a single-char exact match (typing "M" into Shift is already a complete option) and its drop behavior is inconsistent across browsers (verified in the harness). `IdentitySuggestInput` renders the bare `EDIT_INPUT`-metric input (with `data-navid` so keyboard nav still finds it) PLUS a compact glass dropdown (`bg-popover/95 backdrop-blur-lg`, `animate-fade-in`) anchored under the cell: as you type it prefix-filters the valid set case-insensitively (space-insensitive, e.g. `tnk1`); **↓/↑** move the highlight (consumed — the grid does NOT navigate cells); **Enter/Tab WITH a genuine prefix match on a NON-EMPTY query (or an explicit arrow pick)** accept the highlighted option (`flushSync(onChange)` so the explicit commit reads the accepted value) then BUBBLE so the grid commits + moves (canonical Excel flow); an **UNMATCHED query (e.g. "XY")** shows NO dropdown and Tab/Enter commits the RAW text (it is NEVER silently snapped to a valid value); **click** an item accepts + commits; **Esc** closes the dropdown first (value kept), a second Esc bubbles → grid reverts to the snapshot. **CLEAR-TO-EMPTY (regression fix):** the auto-accept gate is `hasMatch = q !== '' && matches.length > 0` — it must be FALSE on an EMPTY query, otherwise `options.filter(o => o.startsWith(''))` = ALL options would auto-snap a cleared field back to the first option on Tab/Enter, so the operator could NEVER clear a Grade/Source/Shift identity cell. Emptying a cell + Tab/Enter (or selecting it + Delete/Backspace then committing) now commits EMPTY → `normalizeIdentity('')='' `→ `upsertDraft(slotKey, { shift|grade|source: '' })` clears that draft field. All three identity cells clear consistently (verified in a harness, W6 + W7). On commit the value is normalized against the allowed set (`normalizeIdentity`, case-insensitive, tank aliases `t1`/`tnk1`→`TNK 1`). **Recognized/tagged confirmation (Issue 2b):** the STATIC identity cell renders a committed value via `renderRecognizedIdentity(col, value, plantView)` — a value that IS a valid member of its set gets the SAME canonical treatment the REAL rows use (Shift → the colored `SHIFT_LETTER` letter + `SHIFT_LABEL` title; Grade → the `GRADE_CHIP` pill; Source → mono-bold), so the operator can SEE it tagged as the DB value; a non-empty value that is NOT recognized renders in a clear **unrecognized amber** style (`text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400`, `title="Not a recognized value"`). `CellEditor` gained two optional props that power this — `renderEdit` (custom edit-mode element = the typeahead) and `renderValue` (custom static render = the recognized styling) — while still owning the static↔edit toggle + value-accessor registration. `identityOptions()`/`isRecognizedIdentity()` are the shared valid-set helpers. **Recv Date** is typed (normalized via `normalizeTypedDate` on commit) and starts **EMPTY** (placeholder only — NO prod_date prefill). Then editable equipment/Bagging weights. Committing a weight **validates the identity is complete** (shift + grade + source + recv) — if not, a gentle `toast.warning` and the value is kept but NOT staged. A complete-identity filler stages **one INSERT per non-empty weight column** (so C1 + C2 on one filler row = 2 events sharing the pull dims), with `prod_date` = the day, dims from the filler, `plant_code` = `plantView` (auto), batch from `selectedPeriod`, disposition/equipment from the column. **Crushers/Kilns** stage directly; **Bagging** opens a small warehouse/side/flec `BaggingMetaPopover`. Drafts live in a `drafts` Map keyed by a **STABLE, PARENT-OWNED, DETERMINISTIC slot id** (`${prodDate}#slot-{i}`, positional per day) — NOT a child `React.useId`. **This is the Issue-1 (duplicate-row) fix:** previously each `FillerRow` self-generated its draft id via `React.useId()` AND the parent rendered filled drafts FIRST (reordered ahead of the empty slots), so committing a blank slot made React MOVE the typed instance to a new index while ALSO mounting a fresh instance at the prepended draft index → two live rows shared one `slotKey` → duplicate `data-navid` → the typed value appeared in TWO rows with BOTH highlighted. Now `DateSection` assigns every slot (empty OR filled) a persistent positional id, passes it DOWN to `FillerRow` as the `slotKey` prop, and renders rows in **slot-id ORDER (not filled-first)** — `[...slotIds.map(sid → draftByKey.get(sid)), ...orphanDrafts]` — so a slot that goes empty→filled keeps its id AND its render position → React preserves the ONE instance (no second mount, no navid collision, no state bleed into a neighbouring slot). `slotCount = max(MIN_DAY_ROWS−dayBody, maxOccupiedDraftSlot+2, #filledDrafts+1) + drawer extras`, guaranteeing ≥1 trailing empty slot once a slot fills; a `FillerRow` effect clears its local identity state when its `draft` prop drops to null (Discard, which doesn't remount). Verified in a public harness: typing into a blank slot + Tab yields exactly ONE row with the value, ONE active ring, and ZERO duplicate `data-navid` (querySelectorAll count = 1), for both W6 and W7. `draftToDirtyRows()` converts a complete draft → its INSERT rows, merged into the same save payload. **Add-row drawer (hover-reveal):** the `MIN_DAY_ROWS` rectangle of empty slots stays as-is, but the previous **auto-grow-on-fill is REMOVED** — a small "+ Add row" pill at the top edge of the day footer's label cell (hidden until the day is hovered, `group-hover/day`, opacity-only, no layout shift) appends one input row to THAT day via per-day `extraRows` state (`slotCount = padSlots + extraRows`). **"Add day"** — a toolbar `AddDayButton` popover takes a typed date (normalized; validated as a real date, not already shown, within `selectedPeriod`'s month when resolvable) → spawns a NEW empty outlined day block (`addedDays`, sorted chronologically; empty added days save nothing). **Draft rows render flat** until save+refresh, when they become real merged rows. The **Date `rowSpan` covers real + draft + filler-slot rows** (footer separate) so the grid stays rectangular. `hasChanges`/"N unsaved" include draft-derived inserts; **Discard** clears all edit + draft + added-day state. **Excel-fidelity cell behavior (Phase 2.1):** (a) **identity typeahead** (custom `IdentitySuggestInput` dropdown, above); (b) **recv blank = empty** (no prefill); (c) **placeholder** is visible when empty+unfocused but **vanishes the instant the cell is focused** (placeholder attr blanked on focus, restored on empty blur) on every editable identity/recv/weight input; (d) **uniform row height** — a single `ROW_H` (`h-7`) on EVERY `<tr>` (real data, filler/input, AND the daily-total footer — its label dropped `py-1`→`py-0.5`), all cells `align-middle`; (e) **no height expansion on edit** — the STATIC cell render AND the shared inline `EditInput` both use `EDIT_INPUT` (`text-[11px]` / `px-1.5` / `leading-none` / `bg-transparent` / no border/ring/outline / `box-border` / `h-full` / `[min-height:0]` / `appearance-none`), so switching to edit shows only the caret and never grows the row. **CELL-HOST HEIGHT (regression fix):** because `EDIT_INPUT` uses `h-full` (height:100%) and a percentage height only resolves against a parent with a *definite* height, every `<td>` that hosts a `CellEditor` (the real-row + filler weight cells' `baseClass`, the filler identity `cellBase`, and the filler Recv cell) carries an explicit **`h-7`** — otherwise the `StaticCell` `<div>` collapses (0px when empty, ~11px when filled) inside a content-height `<td>`, leaving the cell visually present but **un-clickable / un-selectable** (a real click lands in the dead band on the bare `<td>`, which has no handler — the exact symptom of the "single-click does nothing" bug). The explicit `h-7` makes `h-full` resolve to the full cell height (the div fills the cell, fully clickable) **without** growing the row past 28px. **KEYBOARD NAVIGATION + CLICK-TO-SELECT (shared Blackwood Table primitives — Phase 3 retrofit):** the Daily Block now uses the SAME canonical interaction model as every other grid via the shared hooks `useGridKeyboardNav` + `useGridEditSession` + the shared `EditInput` (`@/components/shared/grid`, `@/lib/hooks/use-grid-keyboard-nav`, `@/lib/hooks/use-grid-edit-session`). **Two states per cell:** `activeNavId` = the SELECTED cell (active ring), `editingNavId` = the cell currently in EDIT mode. **Every editable cell renders TWO ways:** by default a focusable **STATIC** element (`StaticCell` — a `<div tabIndex=0 role=gridcell>` matching the `EDIT_INPUT` metrics, carrying `data-navid="${rowKey}|${colKey}"`); it mounts the shared `EditInput` ONLY when `editingNavId === navid`. The `CellEditor` wrapper owns the static↔edit toggle and registers each cell's thin **get / set / commit** accessors with the grid (for snapshot/seed/revert AND explicit commit) — each cell still owns its OWN value state + commit logic, so the entire save path (onEditWeight / onStageInsert / draft upserts / InsertPopover / BaggingMetaPopover) is untouched. **EXPLICIT-COMMIT (regression fix — the Save bar never appeared):** the cell's real commit is run EXPLICITLY through the registry (`commitCell(navid)`), NOT via the editing input's `onBlur`. In React 19 / Next 16 the focus/blur (`focusout`) handler is dropped when the editing input UNMOUNTS in the same render batch — and the grid unmounts the input the instant it moves focus (Tab/Enter) or switches the active cell (click-away), so the onBlur-commit silently never fired (no draft pushed, no `modified`/`deleted` set → `dirtyCount` stayed 0 → no Save/Discard bar, for EVERY edit kind: new filler pull, existing-weight edit, and clear-to-delete). Now `edit.commit` (Tab/Enter) and `onActivate` (click-away to another cell) BOTH call `commitCell(editingNavId)` before clearing edit mode; `editingNavIdRef` (a ref mirror of `editingNavId`) is read in those callbacks so the commit isn't a side effect inside a state updater (StrictMode-safe). The input's `onBlur` is kept only as a harmless idempotent fallback. **CANONICAL gestures:** **CLICK** (mousedown) on a static cell = SELECT only (ring, NO edit) + focus; **TYPE a printable char / F2 / DOUBLE-CLICK** = begin editing (type-over seeds the char); **ESC** = revert to the pre-edit snapshot (the EditInput's `escapedRef` suppresses the blur-commit), stay selected; **ENTER / TAB** = commit (the cell's real commit fires EXPLICITLY via `commitCell` in `edit.commit` — NOT via onBlur, which is dropped on unmount; see EXPLICIT-COMMIT) + move; **ARROWS / TAB / ENTER** (not editing) = navigate. Nav uses `createDomOrderNavResolver` (`containerRef`=scroll container, `navColOrder`=`NAV_COL_ORDER`, `parseId`=`parseNavId`, `findColInAdjacentRow`=a thin adapter reproducing the same-lane-preference / nearest-by-`NAV_COL_ORDER` fallback against the shared `DomNavCell[]` signature). The resolver reads **`[data-navid]`** (NOT `input[data-navid]`) so it finds the STATIC cells too — this is what makes click-to-select navigate. `onAfterMove` focuses the resolved `[data-navid]` element via `CSS.escape` so keyboard nav follows visually. **Enter-anchor** is provided by the shared hook (`enableEnterAnchor: true`, `laneOf` = colKey): the first Tab of a run records the colKey and a later plain Enter returns to it on the next row. **Navigation SKIPS read-only/locked cells** automatically — collision-locked weight cells (🔒) and real-row identity cells render as non-input `<span>`s with **no `data-navid`** → absent from the resolver's cell list (and `resolver.isEditable` is false for any element bearing `data-locked`); the footer/headers are never navigable. Click + keyboard share ONE active-cell model (`activeNavId`, set by a static cell's mousedown/focus → `onActivate`, which also exits any OTHER cell's edit). **ACTIVE-CELL HIGHLIGHT (matches the ledger):** the cell whose `data-navid === activeNavId` gets `ACTIVE_RING` = `ring-2 ring-primary ring-inset` (the SAME token the ledger's selected cell uses in `production-ledger-grid.tsx`) + `z-20`. An INSET ring is a box-shadow → **NO layout space**, so the highlighted cell stays the exact same height as every other (honors the no-expand rule). **Static — no transition** (CLAUDE.md cell-selection rule). It sits ON TOP of any dirty tint — the later `ring-*` wins ring color/width while the dirty `bg-*/10` fill remains, so both read together (e.g. an active amber-modified cell = amber fill + primary ring). `z-20` lifts the active cell ABOVE the z-10 frozen columns (`.frozen-col`/`.frozen-edge`) so the ring is never clipped behind a sticky neighbor on horizontal scroll; it follows clicks AND keyboard nav. Applied to all editable cells (weight on real+filler, filler identity/recv); locked/read-only cells never become active so never highlight. **Bagging + popover during nav:** Tab/Enter into a bagging weight commits; a positive value opens the warehouse/side/flec popover (Radix moves focus into it) — the popover briefly interrupts the Tab flow, and after confirm/cancel the user clicks/Tabs to resume (focus isn't trapped, the active cell isn't lost). **Known gaps (boss-sheet parity):** NO "RECV CCC" column (no CCC bucket — only C1–C4 / RK1–RK4 / FLEC); NO tank-composition string (Source is its own row per location, and Recv Date is now its own pivot column). Cenapro has no audit-log trigger (writes don't produce `audit_logs`); app rows can't be tagged with a distinct `provenance` through the view (needs a migration). Do not fabricate either. **MOBILE (Archetype E phone-summary):** the frozen matrix can't shrink to a phone (5 frozen identity columns alone = 476px > screen), so the `<div ref={gridScrollRef}>` matrix is `hidden … sm:block` (desktop-only, editing stays desktop by decree) and a `sm:hidden` **`CenaproDailyBlockSummaryMobile`** (local to `production-daily-block.tsx`) renders instead: a **per-day "Daily total" strip** (horizontally-scrollable chips reusing `group.daily.total`) + a **stacked per-row list** — one card per day showing `daily.total`/`daily.subTotal`/`daily.bagging` (the load-bearing footer values) then the pivot leaves flattened (`shift · grade · source · recvDate · recvRow.total`, styled with the same `SHIFT_LETTER`/`GRADE_CHIP`/`pillBase` tokens). Built entirely from the existing `groups` (`buildDateGroups`) pivot — **no number is recomputed**; there is no ₱ anywhere in Cenapro. **EXPORTS the pivot engine for reuse (Phase 2B mechanical extraction):** `buildDateGroups` + `SOURCE_SETS` (re-exported from `production-sources.ts`) + the pivot types (`DateGroup`/`ShiftBlock`/`GradeBlock`/`SourceBlock`/`RecvRow`/`DailyTotals`/`CellMap`/`CellSlot`/`EditColumn`/`EquipmentCode`) + `EQUIPMENT_CODES`/`BAGGING`/`ACTIVE_EQUIP`/`CRUSHER_COUNT` + the format helpers (`fmt`/`formatDayLabel`/`formatRecvLabel`) + the column geometry (`W_*`/`LEFT_*`/`IDENTITY_WIDTH`) + the visual tokens (`GRID`/`GROUP`/`BOX`/`ROW_H`/`CELL_PAD`/`SHIFT_LETTER`/`SHIFT_LABEL`/`GRADE_CHIP`/`pillBase`) + **`MIN_DAY_ROWS`** (the shared per-day filler floor, so the focus + endless views pad to the SAME minimum) — the endless read-only pivot renderer (`production-endless-pivots.tsx`) imports these so both variants share ONE pivot + geometry + token source (a width/token change propagates to both). `PlantView`/`SOURCE_SETS` themselves now live in the pure `production-sources.ts` (imported here). **Phase 3b** also EXPORTS its editing primitives for the endless pivot to reuse (`IdentitySuggestInput`, `InsertPopover`, `BaggingMetaPopover`, `renderRecognizedIdentity`, `normalizeIdentity`, `columnDisposition`, `parseWeight`, `identityOptions`, `isRecognizedIdentity`, `draftIdentityComplete`/`draftHasWeights`, `EDIT_COLUMNS`, `NAV_COL_ORDER`/`navColIndex`, `MONTHS`, types `FillerDraft`/`BaggingMeta`/`StagedInsert`/`NavColKey`), and takes an **`onDirtyChange?`** prop that lifts its dirty signal to `production-ledger-grid.tsx` so the focus-scope axis controls guard a switch that would silently discard the block's edits (the audit's severe finding). |
| `production/production-sources.ts` | **Pure module (NO `'use client'`, no server tag)** — the single source of truth for the plant SOURCE filter: `PlantView` (`'W6'\|'W7'`) + `SOURCE_SETS` (`W6 = {TNK 1..4, W6}`, `W7 = {W7}`; FLEC/DVO excluded from both). Lives in its own pure module precisely so the **server** action `fetchDailyPivotWindow` can filter server-side WITHOUT importing the `'use client'` daily-block component across the boundary. Imported by `production-daily-block.tsx` (re-exports `PlantView`), `actions.ts`, and `production-endless-pivots.tsx`. |
| `production/use-daily-pivot-window.ts` | Client hook — **`useDailyPivotWindow(initialWindow, plant)`**: the DAY-granular sibling of `useLedgerWindow` for the endless W6/W7 pivots. Accumulates a flat, oldest-first EVENT window (the renderer pivots it with `buildDateGroups` into day-blocks) and OWNS react-virtuoso's `firstItemIndex` in **DAY-BLOCK units** (a backward fetch decrements it by the number of prepended DISTINCT prod_dates — exact because the server pre-filters the plant source set, so every distinct day = one block; prepend + decrement batch together → no scroll jump). Seeded from the server-prefetched `initialWindow`; holds `events`/`firstItemIndex`/`hasOlder`/`hasNewer`/`loadingOlder`/`loadingNewer`/`notice`; exposes `fetchOlder()` (day-cursor = the oldest loaded `prod_date`, prepends), `fetchNewer()` (day-cursor = the newest loaded `prod_date`, appends), `reset(anchor)` (re-seed). Per-direction ref guards ignore concurrent/exhausted calls; de-dups by `id`; calls `fetchDailyPivotWindow` directly; errors → `errorToast`. **Phase 3b adds `refreshWindow()`** — after a Save, re-reads the loaded day-window fresh (period-anchored on the current top event, restores prior day-depth), so weight edits / deletes / pull re-tags / new pulls land + re-bucket without a full reload. |
| `production/production-endless-pivots.tsx` | Client — **the Endless W6/W7 Daily Pivots (Phase 2B, READ-ONLY)**: the pivot analog of the endless ledger. ONE continuous, virtualized, oldest-first view of the ENTIRE production history, but paginated by WHOLE prod-days and rendered as VIRTUALIZED DAY-BLOCKS scrolled in both directions across month boundaries. Built on `react-virtuoso`'s `TableVirtuoso`: **each day-block is ONE virtualized item** — the item's `<tr>` holds a single full-`colSpan` `<td>` containing a **nested read-only `<table>`** whose internal `rowSpan`s (the frozen 5-col identity Date/Shift/Grade/Source/Recv) render inside it; virtuoso measures each block's variable height. **ONE shared, opaque, frozen 2-tier header** (`fixedHeaderContent` = `PivotHeaderRows`, `TableHead` = `frozen-row bg-muted` z-20) sits over all blocks; the outer table's colgroup and every nested block's colgroup are IDENTICAL (`PivotColGroup`, `width:max-content`+`minWidth`) so columns line up under horizontal scroll, and the frozen identity cells (`.frozen-col`/`.frozen-corner`/`.frozen-edge`, cumulative `left` offsets) stick to the shared `Scroller` (custom, `overflow-x:auto`).
