## Price gating (Cenapro is no longer ₱-free — 2026-08-04)

Cenapro carried **no money at all** until RC Deliveries landed, which is why older notes in this file say there is nothing to gate. That is now true only of the production and flec screens. **`cenapro.rc_delivery` is a money table**, and it is the input to liquidation, so the platform rule in `CLAUDE.md` applies in full.

**Seven fields are gated:** `base_price_php_kg`, `price_adjustment_php_kg`, `price_php_kg`, `price_formula`, `total_price_php`, `sheet_total_php`, `sheet_total_matches`. `price_formula` is in the list because `=39.5+2.7` states the price as plainly as the number does; `sheet_total_php`/`sheet_total_matches` are in it because the workbook's own printed total is still a total.

- **`canViewPrices()` from `lib/auth.ts` is the ONE helper** — it derives the effective role from `getUserRole()`, so it respects the dev impersonation cookie (an Owner "viewing as Production" is denied). Never re-derive it with an inline `profiles.select('role')` lookup.
- **The nulling happens SERVER-SIDE, before the payload is returned** — `stripPrices()` in `deliveries/types.ts`, called from every fetch in `deliveries/actions.ts`. The network response is the leak; hiding the columns client-side would not be gating.
- A `canViewPrices` boolean rides down for conditional render, and when it is false `buildColumns()` **omits the two ₱ columns entirely** rather than blanking them — so the grid's keyboard coordinate space has no unreachable holes and the table's min-width stays honest.
- **The write path is gated too:** `saveDeliveries` refuses a patch carrying a ₱ key from a gated viewer (outcome `forbidden`), per receipt rather than silently filtered — a silent drop would look like a successful save that lost the operator's typing.
- **Production is the only role that cannot see prices** (the platform-wide rule; see `components/providers/AUTH.md` → "Price Gating").

## Data path (public `cenapro_*` accessors — NOT `.schema('cenapro')`)
The cenapro data is reached through **accessors that live in the `public` schema**, which PostgREST already exposes:
- a VIEW `public.cenapro_production_events` (16 columns) — read by `fetchProductionEvents`, **written** by `saveProductionEvents` (auto-updatable; see "Production write path").
- two set-returning functions `public.cenapro_flec_balance(...)` / `public.cenapro_flec_ledger(...)` (read-only).
- three opening-balance accessors `public.cenapro_opening_balances(...)` / `public.cenapro_opening_balance_history(...)` (read-only) + **`public.cenapro_set_opening_balance(...)`** (append-only write RPC, `authenticated`/`service_role` only) — see "Opening-balance write path".
- **CCC analysis (2026-08-01):** a writable VIEW `public.cenapro_analysis_samples`, three read-only VIEWs `public.cenapro_ccc_sample_groups` / `_ccc_analysis_daily` / `_ccc_analysis_monthly`, and two RPCs `public.cenapro_save_analysis_sample(...)` (authenticated) / `public.cenapro_import_analysis_samples(jsonb)` (service_role only). **These four views grant SELECT to `authenticated` ONLY — `anon` is revoked**, unlike the older accessors above. See "CCC Analysis — LIVE BACKEND".
- **`cenapro.production_event` writes from the QC Ledger (2026-08-03):** two RPCs, both `authenticated` + `service_role`, `anon` revoked — **`public.cenapro_update_event_weight(...)`** (correct ONE stored weight, compare-and-set) and **`public.cenapro_add_partner_draw(...)`** (add ONE partner draw; partner draws only, flec bagging refused). Plus the read-only trail accessor `public.cenapro_production_event_audit`. See "Adding a partner draw" and "Auditing `cenapro.production_event`".
- **RC Deliveries (2026-08-04) — data layer** (the route `/cenapro/deliveries` is LIVE; its files are listed in `deliveries/CONTEXT.md`): two writable dimension VIEWs `public.cenapro_rc_suppliers` / `public.cenapro_rc_destinations`, the writable fact VIEW `public.cenapro_rc_deliveries` + its child `public.cenapro_rc_delivery_samples`, the read-only enriched VIEW `public.cenapro_rc_delivery_rows`, and three RPCs `public.cenapro_save_rc_delivery(...)` / `public.cenapro_delete_rc_delivery(...)` / `public.cenapro_save_rc_delivery_samples(...)`. **All `authenticated` + `service_role`; `anon` revoked everywhere.** See "RC Deliveries — DATA LAYER" below.
- **Liquidation (2026-08-05) — data layer only, no route yet:** the READ-ONLY views `public.cenapro_rc_banks` / `cenapro_rc_bank_accounts` / `cenapro_rc_payments` / `cenapro_rc_payment_audit` / **`cenapro_rc_supplier_balances`** / **`cenapro_rc_supplier_group_balances`**, plus five RPCs `public.cenapro_save_rc_bank(...)` / `cenapro_save_rc_bank_account(...)` / `cenapro_save_rc_payment(...)` / `cenapro_delete_rc_payment(...)` (SOFT) / `cenapro_restore_rc_payment(...)`. **None of these views is writable** — the RPCs are the only write door. All are ₱-bearing and belong behind `canViewPrices()`. See "Liquidation" below.
- **Allocation (2026-08-06, Step 4) — data layer only, NO UI:** the READ-ONLY views **`public.cenapro_rc_delivery_settlement`** (one row per receipt — `allocated_php`, `balance_php`, `settlement_status`) / **`public.cenapro_rc_payment_state`** (`unallocated_php`, `is_advance` — the cash-advance list) / **`public.cenapro_rc_payment_allocations`** (the edges), plus three RPCs `public.cenapro_save_rc_payment_allocations(...)` (whole-block replace, gated on the PAYMENT's `row_version`) / `cenapro_allocate_delivery_to_payment(...)` (the delivery-first door, built on top of it) / `cenapro_restore_rc_payment_allocation(...)`. **`public.cenapro_delete_rc_delivery` now takes a third argument** `p_release_allocations boolean DEFAULT false` and refuses with `has_allocations` when money is assigned — the existing 2-arg caller is unaffected. The balance accessors gained `advance_php` + 3 siblings. All ₱-bearing. See "Allocation" below.
- **Opening balances (2026-08-06) — data layer only, no UI yet:** the READ-ONLY views **`public.cenapro_rc_supplier_opening_balances`** (current revision per supplier) and **`cenapro_rc_supplier_opening_balance_history`** (every revision, `is_current`), plus the **INSERT-only** appender `public.cenapro_set_rc_supplier_opening_balance(p_supplier_code, p_as_of_date, p_opening_balance_php, p_note)` — **there is no update rpc and no delete rpc**, a correction is a new revision. `cenapro_rc_supplier_balances` gained 23 columns (opening / carried / all-time / windowed-unpriced) and `cenapro_rc_supplier_group_balances` 22; **every pre-existing column kept its name, type, position AND value**. Both are ₱-bearing. See "Opening balances" below.

Reads are granted to `authenticated` + `anon` (writes to `authenticated`), so the **standard Supabase client reaches them directly** — `supabase.from('cenapro_production_events')` and `supabase.rpc('cenapro_flec_balance'|'cenapro_flec_ledger'|'cenapro_opening_balances'|'cenapro_opening_balance_history'|'cenapro_set_opening_balance', …)`. No `.schema('cenapro')`, no structural casts.

**Why this path (and not exposing the `cenapro` schema):** flipping PostgREST's "Exposed schemas" toggle to add `cenapro` would surface the *entire* schema's API surface and is a project-wide setting. Wrapping just the objects the UI needs as `public.cenapro_*` accessors keeps the blast radius minimal while still giving fully-typed, first-class client access.

**Types:** `types.ts` derives all row shapes from the generated `types/supabase.ts` (no hand-written interfaces), so they auto-update on every `supabase gen types typescript --linked`:
- `ProductionEventRow = Database['public']['Views']['cenapro_production_events']['Row']`
- `FlecBalanceRow = Database['public']['Functions']['cenapro_flec_balance']['Returns'][number]`
- `FlecLedgerRow = Database['public']['Functions']['cenapro_flec_ledger']['Returns'][number]`
- `OpeningBalanceRow = Database['public']['Functions']['cenapro_opening_balances']['Returns'][number]`
- `OpeningBalanceHistoryRow = Database['public']['Functions']['cenapro_opening_balance_history']['Returns'][number]`
- `OpeningBalanceCellChange` — hand-authored payload interface (one changed STARTING cell: `warehouse`/`grade`/`side`/`effectiveDate`/`count`) for `saveOpeningBalances`; the only non-derived type, since it's a UI→action contract, not a DB row.

Notes:
- The production `.select(...)` column list MUST be a **single string literal** (not `+`-concatenated) — the typed PostgREST client parses it at the type level to infer the row shape; concatenation defeats inference and falls back to an error type.
- The upsert payload is typed against `Database['public']['Views']['cenapro_production_events']['Insert']` (the auto-updatable view's generated Insert block; identical to Update). New rows omit `id` entirely so the base default generates it.
- `formatDisposition(disposition: string | null, …)` is typed loosely (string) to consume the generated row types directly; at runtime it's always one of the three known kinds, unknown values render as-is.
- The old `ProductionDailyRow` stopgap interface (for `view_production_daily`) was **removed** — it had no consumer and no matching `public.cenapro_*` accessor in the generated types. Re-derive it from generated types if/when that view is surfaced and exposed.
- **Live data confirmed from Node** via the anon key (2026-06-01): `cenapro_production_events` count = 752; `cenapro_flec_balance(WHSE 7, 2026-03-10)` 3X50/RS `current_flec` = 56; `cenapro_flec_ledger(WHSE 7, 2026-03-10)` = 100 rows, all 16 columns; `cenapro_opening_balances(WHSE 7, 2026-03-10)` = 3X50/RS 53 + 2X6/LS 26 (the seeds). The browser pages are auth-gated (a `GET /cenapro` returns 307 → login when unauthenticated), so this Node check is the runtime proof short of a logged-in session.

## Dependencies
- `@/lib/supabase/server` — `createClient()` (server Supabase client; reads the public `cenapro_*` view/RPCs via `.from()` / `.rpc()`, **writes** the auto-updatable production view via `.upsert()` / `.delete()`, and writes opening balances via the append-only `cenapro_set_opening_balance` RPC).
- `@/types/supabase` — generated `Database` type; `types.ts` derives the cenapro row shapes from it; the production upsert payload uses the view's `Insert` block; the five flec/opening RPC row shapes derive from the function `Returns`.
- `@/components/shared/grid/{GridCell,SelectCell,DatePickerCell,EditInput,EDIT_INPUT}` — the shared Excel-style cells: `GridCell` (display/edit modes, selection feedback), `SelectCell` (categorical dropdown cell) and `DatePickerCell` (native date cell + `formatDateShort`) which the production ledger IMPORTS from the shared barrel (local copies promoted to `components/shared/grid/` in Phase 0, deleted from the ledger in Phase 2 — byte-identical, no drift), and **`EditInput` + `EDIT_INPUT`** which the Daily Block (`production-daily-block.tsx`) IMPORTS (its local `EditInput`/`EDIT_INPUT` were the Phase-0 promotion source and have been deleted; the Daily Block's `CellEditor` mounts the shared `EditInput` in edit mode). See `components/shared/grid/CONTEXT.md`.
- `@/lib/hooks/use-grid-keyboard-nav` (`useGridKeyboardNav` + `createDomOrderNavResolver` + `DomNavCell`) and `@/lib/hooks/use-grid-edit-session` (`useGridEditSession`) — the Daily Block's keyboard state machine + edit session (Phase 3). DOM-order resolver over `[data-navid]`; locked cells carry no `data-navid` so nav skips them.
- `@/lib/hooks/{use-cell-selection,use-clipboard-copy,use-cell-delete}` — cell range selection, Ctrl+C TSV copy, Backspace/Delete clear (shared with RC IN / RC OUT / ICTC daily ledger).
- `@/lib/hooks/{use-grid-keyboard-nav,use-grid-edit-session,use-grid-paste}` — the shared Blackwood Table grid primitives. The production ledger + the endless sheet's draft entry zone consume `useGridKeyboardNav` (keyboard state machine + `createCoordinateNavResolver`, wrapped per-grid to clamp ArrowLeft to col 1 and — ledger only — to gate inline editing to typable columns via a custom `isEditable`) and `useGridEditSession` (owns `isEditing` + pre-edit snapshot + start/revert/commit; the draft entry zone passes `onAfterCommit` = its typed-date normalizer; the ledger keeps a CUSTOM revert that also un-dirties a reverted modified-existing row). The draft entry zone also uses `useGridPaste`; the ledger keeps its OWN `handleSmartPaste` because its paste must mark pasted existing rows `modified` (the shared paste hook doesn't touch `_state`). Home/End (both grids) and single-cell concerns are handled in thin container wrappers. `enableEnterAnchor`: **true** for the draft entry zone (Tab-then-Enter returns to the run's lane — the old `enterAnchorColRef` behavior, now from the shared hook), **false** for the ledger (plain Enter drops straight down).
- `@/lib/paste-utils` — `parseExcelDate`, `trimCellValue` for paste-from-Excel cleaning, and **`normalizeTypedDate(input, defaultYear)`** for Excel-style commit-time auto-transcription of TYPED shorthand dates (`"6/2"`→`yyyy-MM-dd`, injecting `defaultYear` when the year is omitted; invalid/empty passed through unchanged) — used by the draft entry zone's date cells + `mapBulkRowToDirty` safety net.
- `@/lib/toast` — `errorToast()` (persistent + Copy; HARD RULE) for all save/validation errors.
- `@/components/ui/{table,select,card,dropdown-menu,button,input,popover,collapsible,badge,dialog}` — Shadcn primitives (`dropdown-menu` powers the production `SelectCell` + header filters; the Flec Inventory page uses `select` for the warehouse picker, `popover` for the per-cell STARTING history, and `collapsible` for the whole-warehouse history panel).
- `@/lib/utils` — `cn()`.
- `react-virtuoso` — `TableVirtuoso` (the endless sheet's virtualizer; chosen for its `firstItemIndex` prepend-anchor primitive — `@tanstack/react-virtual`, used elsewhere for full-dataset renders, has no equivalent). Consumed ONLY by `production-endless-sheet.tsx`.
- `date-fns` — `format`, `parseISO`, `isValid` (date display — `yyyy-MM-dd`).
- `sonner` — success/info toasts (paste, save, copy); errors go through `errorToast` per HARD RULE.
- `lucide-react` — icons.

> ✅ **Shipped 2026-08-01.** Renzo evaluated four candidate designs at the throwaway
> `/cenapro/analysis-drafts/{a,b,c,d}` routes, picked **A for entry** and **D for reading**,
> and the two were promoted here. **The draft tree, `lib/cenapro/ccc-analysis-draft.ts` and the
> five draft breadcrumb entries are DELETED** — do not resurrect them; the drafts' visual
> decisions (round-2 column widths, Month+Year dropdowns, recharts, spreadsheet keyboard nav,
> `Σ DAY TOTAL` rule-offs, the opaque frozen month footer, no stacked bar charts) all survive
> in the promoted routes. B and C were rejected and are gone entirely.

Two routes over one shared **`?m=YYYY-MM`** month axis, cross-linked both ways:

| Route | Role | Scope | Writes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `/cenapro/qc` — **QC Ledger** | ENTRY. The CCC-CI ANALYSIS sheet as a dense 12-column grid; the four metric columns are editable, everything left of them is reference. | **`scope='all'`** — the entry surface must show every receipt an operator can type against, DVO included. | Yes — `cenapro_save_analysis_sample`, one RPC per sample group. |
| `/cenapro/qc/breakdown` — **QC Breakdown** | READING. Monthly + daily weighted analytics, four metric trend panels. | **`scope='ex_dvo'`** — the sheet's headline framing. | No. Read-only by design. |

### Files
| File | Role |
|------|------|
| `qc/page.tsx` | Server — resolves `?m=` (via `resolveQcMonth`), then awaits `loadQcLedgerData` + **`loadQcDrawOptions`** in ONE `Promise.all` (independent reads, not two round trips in series), renders `QcLedgerClient`. `force-dynamic`. |
| `qc/data.ts` | **The ONE read path for BOTH routes.** Not a server action (no `'use server'`, no mutations) — plain async functions the two `page.tsx`s await. `loadQcMonthKeys()` (the month picker's axis, from the monthly view), `loadQcLedgerData(month, monthKeys)` (four parallel reads: sample groups + production events + `_analysis_daily scope='all'` + `_analysis_monthly scope='all'`, assembled into day blocks), `loadQcBreakdownData(month, monthKeys)` (monthly + daily at `scope='ex_dvo'`), and **`loadQcDrawOptions()` (2026-08-03)** — the "Add draw" composer's dimension lists (`QcDrawOptions`: `sources` / `crushers` / `kilns` / `grades` / `shifts` / `warehouses` / `sides`), see "Where the ADD form's options come from" below. Exports `QcDraw` / `QcGroup` / `QcLedgerDay` / `QcMonthRow` / `QcDayRow` / `QcDrawOptions` / the two `*Data` shapes. **The view IS the grain:** groups are built from `cenapro_ccc_sample_groups` rows and the production-event draws are hung off them by `sampleGroupKey(date, canonToken(src), effectiveWhseKey(row))`; a draw whose group the view does not carry (impossible today — the view is built from those rows) synthesizes an orphan group rather than silently dropping tonnage. |
| `qc/draw-entry-rows.tsx` | Client (2026-08-04) — **`DraftRow`** + the draft model (`DraftDraw`, `makeBlankDraft`/`makeBlankDrafts`, `isMeaningfulDraft`, `isSendableDraft`, `draftBlocker`, `draftMetrics`, `draftToInput`, `derivedPlant`, **`plantOverride`/`effectivePlant`**, `draftGroupKey`, `findDraftReadingConflicts`, `COL_COUNT`). Entry happens IN THE ROWS. **Replaced `add-draw-panel.tsx` (955 lines, DELETED).** Renzo, on seeing the panel: *"I don't like it in general. When we do add draw, it should create 10 new empty rows underneath the latest row. It should act like an excel sheet where we can input whatever on the new empty rows."* A slip is transcribed by reading down it; a form beside the grid makes you look in two places and re-find your place after every line. **SECOND PASS (2026-08-04) — every cell but PLANT is typed.** Renzo: *"i want all the cells to be editable. Same behavior as production ledger. Currently its blocking me from manipulating certain columns."* **Typable:** date · prod · shift · grade · whse · side · bags · src · mach · wt · **BD · ASH · GRIT · MC**. WHSE/SIDE/BAGS were `disabled` until SRC read `FLEC` — a pure UI gate with nothing behind it (the RPC takes all three directly and `draftBlocker`/the server/the RPC already refuse them on a non-FLEC row) that made typing down a row impossible, because those three columns sit **before** SRC and you could not reach the cell that unlocked them. The four metric cells replaced the single `colSpan` status cell; the sequencing that makes them safe lives in `addQcDraws` (draw first, then the reading against the group the RPC reports). **THIRD PASS (2026-08-04) — `PLANT` is typable too, so nothing is exempt.** Renzo: *"I don't understand how PLANT has to stay this way when it's very much typeable in the production ledger?"* The old rule was one function's choice, not the data model — the RPC INSERTs into `cenapro.production_event`, the same table and the same column the Production ledger writes through an ordinary dropdown, and this screen transcribes **partner slips**, which can legitimately name a plant the source mapping does not predict. The cell is now the SAME `SelectCell` + `plantBadgeClass` treatment (imported from the pure `../badges.ts`, never re-styled), and it still hosts the row's `×` remove control — the Plant column widened 60→**88px** to hold badge + chevron + `×`. **Derived by DEFAULT, typed only on purpose:** `DraftDraw.plant` holds the OVERRIDE alone (`''` = follow SRC); the cell displays `effectivePlant()` — the override, else the live `derivedPlant(src)` — and renders a derived value **ghosted** (`border-dashed opacity-60`, same colour family) against a typed one's solid badge, so a row never claims a transcription it does not have. A `— follow SRC` item (an OPTION, not `SelectCell`'s built-in `nullable`, whose "— None" would read as *clear the plant* — the one thing this cell must never mean) puts it back to derived. **`p_plant` is sent ONLY when `plantOverride()` is non-empty**, so a derived value is never echoed back as a supplied one; blank means derive, all the way down to the RPC's own rule, and a phantom sample group stays unreachable. `draftGroupKey` mirrors `coalesce(warehouse, plant)` over the **effective** plant, so an overridden row is bucketed where the server will actually file it. There is deliberately **no client-side plant allowlist** — an unknown code is the RPC's `invalid_key`, shown verbatim. **Dates are FREE TEXT** (`type="date"` is gone): `parseQcDate` on blur/Enter normalizes `6/27` · `6/27/26` · `2026-06-27` · `27 Jun 26` · an Excel serial → `yyyy-MM-dd`, and a bare day-and-month takes the **focused month's year** (`contextYear`, the ledger is month-scoped). Unreadable text is **left exactly as typed** and named by `draftBlocker` — never a silently corrected date. **Blank rows arrive UNDATED (2026-08-04, third pass).** Renzo: *"when you add draw, leave the new cells in both dates column blank."* `makeBlankDraft(anchorDate)` no longer takes a receipt date at all, so a date on a row is always a date someone typed. Two consequences were handled deliberately: (1) **`isMeaningfulDraft` now counts `recvDate`** — it used to skip it on the premise that *"every blank arrives already dated"*, and with that premise dead a row with ONLY a date typed would have looked untouched and been silently dropped by Save; ten fresh blanks still show nothing, because nothing is typed in them. (2) **`anchorDate` stays independent** — it is LAYOUT (which day block a row renders in), fixed at creation, and never derived from `recvDate`; rows added from a day header still render in that day, the toolbar's ten still trail the month, and retyping a date can never make a row jump blocks mid-keystroke. `draftBlocker`'s `needs a date` is now the common *starting* state rather than an error, which reads fine precisely because an untouched blank is not meaningful and therefore gets no status line. **The status line moved to a conditional full-width sub-`<tr>`** under the row (`colSpan={COL_COUNT}`), rendered only when the row has something to say, so it costs no column, hides no refusal, and ten untouched blanks are still ten rows. It is also where a **`notice`** lands (2026-08-04) — a NON-BLOCKING remark returned with a *successful* write (`plant_notice`, and/or the pre-existing sideless-FLEC `notice`; independent, so a FLEC row can carry both). A clean insert is dropped from the draft list and arrives with the refresh; one carrying a remark is KEPT instead, green, marked `drawSaved` so `isSendableDraft` can never re-send it — a notice with nowhere to be read is a notice that was never given. It gets **no confirm round trip**: that machinery belongs to `duplicate_warning` alone. `drawSaved` marks a row whose DRAW landed but whose reading was refused: it keeps the numbers on screen and `isSendableDraft` guarantees it is never re-sent (which would file the receipt twice). Select-ish fields use an `<input list=…>` datalist, so you can type freely OR pick — the same idiom as `production/draft-entry-zone.tsx`. **Why plain inputs and not the grid's coordinate nav:** the saved rows run `createQcNavResolver`, a per-CELL space with a real asymmetry (WT on every row; the four metrics only on a group's LEAD row, with NO coordinate at all on a `〃` sibling). Draft rows have neither groups nor siblings, so folding them in would mean a second row-kind threaded through every branch of the resolver; native Tab order gives the same feel for a fraction of the risk. |
| `qc/actions.ts` | `'use server'` — **THREE** write paths, one per grain. **`saveQcSamples(inputs)`**: one `cenapro_save_analysis_sample` RPC **per sample group**, threading that group's own `sample_row_version` through unchanged (`null` → insert, integer → update, version checked in the same statement as the write). Returns a per-group `SaveQcSampleResult` (`key`, `ok`, `outcome`, `rowVersion`, `message`) so a conflict on one group never blocks the others. Never retries, never force-writes, never re-reads a version to "fix" a conflict. An all-null payload short-circuits to `no_metrics` without a round trip. Metric args are OMITTED when null (every metric param is `DEFAULT NULL`, so omitting clears on the UPDATE path exactly as an explicit null would, and the generated `Args` type declares them `number`). **`saveQcWeights(inputs)` (2026-08-03)**: one `cenapro_update_event_weight` RPC **per DRAW** — `{id, expectedWeightKg, raw}` in, `SaveQcWeightResult` (`id`, `ok`, `outcome`, `weightKg`, `message`) out. Re-runs the shared `parseWeightKg` server-side before spending a round trip, so the client and server can never disagree about what is acceptable. Both revalidate `/cenapro/qc` + `/cenapro/qc/breakdown`; the weight path also revalidates **`/cenapro/production`**, which renders the same row. **`addPartnerDraw(input)` (2026-08-03)**: ONE `cenapro_add_partner_draw` RPC per draw. Takes RAW typed text (`weightRaw`, `flecCountRaw`) and owns the parsing, so the client cannot send a shape the server did not vet. Its pre-flight re-states only what it can be certain of — blank/`FLEC`-as-machine → `wrong_surface`, missing date/source/grade/shift → `invalid_key`, the shared `parseWeightKg`, `prod_date > recv_date`, and the source-conditional bag rule (FLEC ⇒ warehouse + count REQUIRED; any other source ⇒ all three REFUSED by name, never silently dropped) — and returns before touching Supabase, so a typo costs no round trip. Optional args are **OMITTED, not nulled** (an explicit null bag field on a tank draw is a value the RPC would rightly refuse). **`p_plant` (2026-08-04) follows the same idiom for a different reason:** `input.plant` is the operator's OVERRIDE, `if (plant) args.p_plant = plant` sends it only when there is one, and omitted/null/blank all mean "derive from the source" — so the derive path is byte-for-byte the one that was proven live, and no row ever comes back `plant_source: 'supplied'` for a value the machine chose. The plant is deliberately **not validated here** — the RPC owns the list of real plants and refuses an unknown one by name, listing the codes and what the source would have given. Returns `AddQcDrawResult` = the contract's `AddPartnerDrawResult` with `'rpc_error'` added to the outcome union, every field defensively narrowed out of the `jsonb` (including `existing[]`, `sample_group`, and the three plant keys **`plant_source` / `plant_derived` / `plant_notice`**; `plant_code` keeps its key and now carries the EFFECTIVE value). Revalidates `/cenapro/qc` + `/cenapro/qc/breakdown` + `/cenapro/production` on `inserted` only. **Also refuses a `recv_date`/`prod_date` that is not already `yyyy-MM-dd`** (`isIsoDate`) rather than letting the RPC's `date` cast fail with a Postgres message about a column nobody named — and deliberately does NOT parse one itself, because only the client knows which year a bare `6/27` means. **`addQcDraws(rows, groupVersions)` (2026-08-04, second pass)**: still a sequential loop over `addPartnerDraw` (the batch resolver must see each same-day row land), but each row may now carry the lab reading typed beside it and the loop OWNS the draw→reading sequencing. After the inserts it buckets the reading-bearing rows by the sample group **the RPC itself reported** (`sample_group` on the verdict — never a re-derivation), then makes ONE `cenapro_save_analysis_sample` call per bucket, with `expectedRowVersion` taken from the client's `QcGroupVersions` map (the groups on screen) so a draw joining a group that already carries a reading UPDATES it against that version instead of answering `already_exists`. **Two rows in one bucket giving the SAME metric two different values is a `reading_conflict`: refused, both values and the group named, nothing written** — the draws stay (they are real receipts and not in dispute). Different metrics on two rows of one group MERGE — that is a union, not a disagreement. Returns `AddQcDrawRowResult` = `{rowId, result, reading?}` where `reading` is `AddQcReadingResult` (`ok`/`outcome`/`groupKey`/`message`, outcome adds `reading_conflict` + `skipped`). `writeSampleGroup()` is the ONE extracted round trip both this path and `saveQcSamples` go through, so the two can never drift. |
| `qc/qc-ledger-client.tsx` | Client — the entry grid. **15 columns** (`Date · Prod · Sh · Grade · Plant · Whse · Side · Bags · SRC · Mach · WT kg · BD · ASH · GRIT · MC`) at widths `62/62/40/62/88/62/44/52/62/58/88` + `124×4`, table min-width **1,176px** (`MIN_WIDTH` is derived from `COLS`, never hardcoded), so the four analysis columns hold ~42% of the table at EVERY viewport (a `table-fixed` table distributes surplus in proportion to declared widths). **Plant widened 60→88px on 2026-08-04** — on a draft row that cell holds a `SelectCell` (badge + chevron) AND the row's `×`, and `W6/W7` is the widest badge. **FIVE of those columns are editable** — `WT kg` joined the four metrics on 2026-08-03 (`WeightCell`, nav column 0; see "Editing a weight" + "Keyboard model" below), which is why the WT header now reads as `text-foreground` like the metric headers and the WT cells carry the same `bg-sky-500/5` editable tint. Three-tier structure: faint `bg-muted/25` day HEADER (date + sample status), the day's draw rows, a ruled-off **`Σ DAY TOTAL`** (`bg-muted`, `border-t-2 border-t-foreground/45`, bold tabular figures, amber `wtd of NN% sampled` caption under 100%), and a **frozen `Σ <MONTH>` footer** (`<tfoot>` + `.frozen-row-bottom` + `.frozen-edge-top`, solid `bg-muted` — never glass) inside the grid's own `min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto` scrollport, which is also the keyboard host. KPI tiles ×4 with DAILY sparklines and a signed (never colour-coded) delta vs the prior month. Save bar + `Discard`; the toolbar carries the Month/Year picker, the source filter, an **`Add draw` toggle** (`hidden sm:inline-flex` — the grid it writes into is itself desktop-only), the logged/kg/coverage chips and the quiet **`QC Breakdown →`** link. **Adding (2026-08-03):** the grid and `<AddDrawPanel>` sit SIDE BY SIDE in one `flex min-h-0 flex-1` row (the grid gains `min-w-0` so it shrinks and scrolls rather than crushing); each day header carries a `+ ADD` button **in its LEFT block**, beside the date — that is what is on screen at the table's default horizontal scroll position — which opens the composer already dated to that day. The toolbar button opens it on today when today is in the month, else the month's last receipt day. A committed draw sets `markedDrawId`, clears a source filter that would have hidden it, and `router.refresh()`es inside a `useTransition` (the panel shows `updating…`); an effect keyed on `days` scrolls `[data-draw-id]` into view once the row actually exists and tints it `bg-primary/10` for 8s — a STATIC tint, never an entrance animation (CLAUDE.md: no row animations). `markedDrawId` is also what `already_exists` → "Open the existing row" sets. Mobile: `hidden sm:flex` grid + `NarrowScreenNotice`, and no entry affordance at all. **Save-bar controls, rationalised 2026-08-04 (second pass).** Renzo: *"we are not able to cancel adding a draw. Just gives us an option to save and no cancel."* He was right — `Discard` rendered only on `totalPending > 0`, and `totalPending` counts drafts that are meaningful AND unblocked, so immediately after clicking `Add draw` there were ten blank rows, `totalPending === 0` and **no way out of add mode at all**. There are now exactly TWO controls, split by WHAT they throw away: **`Discard edits`** (`totalDirty > 0`) clears the unsaved readings + weights on SAVED rows and nothing else; **`Cancel adding`** renders on `addingDraws` ALONE — always available — exits add mode and removes every draft row, confirming through an `AlertDialog` naming the count only when a draft has real typing in it (ten blanks just close). The old ghost **`Clear N empty rows`** was REMOVED as a third near-synonym: a blank row costs nothing on save, per-row removal is the `×` in the PLANT cell, and Cancel covers "get me out of here". `contextYear` (the focused month's year) is derived here and threaded to every draft row — it is what a bare `6/27` means. `findDraftReadingConflicts` BLOCKS the whole save before anything leaves the browser when two typed rows would give one sample group two different readings (the same refusal `addQcDraws` makes server-side over the RPC's own answer — client-side it is a courtesy that costs no round trip, and a mirror that drifts can only cost a spurious or late refusal, never a merged reading). |
